# Storefront v2 translation review record

[Planning index](README.md) · [Storefront v2 rollout](storefront-rollout.md) · [Canonical translations](../translations/README.md) · [Field map](../translations/storefront-field-map.md) · [Glossary](../translations/glossary.md)

## Method and limits

- **What was done.** On 2026-11-24 the storefront-v2 English listing ([en.md](../translations/en.md), 78 fields) was translated into all 44 non-English locales by a workflow of 88 agent runs: 44 dedicated translator agents, each followed by an independent reviewer agent that compared the catalog with the English, edited it and ran `storefront.py review-record`. Identities are `storefront-v2-translator-<locale>` and `storefront-v2-reviewer-<locale>`.
- **What it is not.** This is AI translation with independent AI linguistic review. No human and no native speaker has read any of these catalogs. A rating below is the reviewing agent's own judgement of its locale, not an outside assessment.
- **Where the review status lives.** The hash-bound review records in [`translations/reviews/`](../translations/reviews/) bind each review to the exact English source and translation hashes. Editing a field stales its record; `python3 translations/tools/storefront.py check --require-review` is the gate. This document is the narrative record and does not replace those files.
- **How this file was made.** It is generated by script from the reviewers' structured results (one object per locale: rating, fields changed, serious errors found, residual concerns). Reviewer text is verbatim, including process remarks and the reviewers' own reminders that no native speaker was involved. Three mechanical changes only: `*` is escaped for Markdown; the absolute local repository path is shortened to a repository-relative one; and entries that only state that no errors were found are not counted as errors or concerns and are listed separately, in full, as the reviewer's overall statement.
- **How to read the counts.** "Serious errors fixed" counts the entries a reviewer returned under that heading, less the no-errors-found statements. It is not a count of confirmed meaning errors: several reviewers say outright that their listed items are moderate or quality fixes (for example `eo`, `nb`, `bg`, `sr-Cyrl`, `tr`, `pt-BR`), and one entry can cover several fields. "Residual concerns" likewise includes process notes.
- **A note on the reviewers' briefs.** Several reviewers remark that their catalog already held a storefront-v2 draft rather than the v1 text their brief described. That is the expected order of the workflow (the translator ran first); the remarks are kept as written.

## Patterns worth a human check

Derived by reading all 44 results. Locale lists name the reviewers who raised the point; a locale missing from a list was not flagged, which is not the same as checked and clear.

- **"No in-app purchases" on the 28-character overlay and 32-character trailer line.** The store's own term for in-app purchases does not fit beside "no ads" in most languages, so `shot_overlay_fair_deal` and `trailer_line_4` carry a shorter form than the body text. Bare "purchases", which is wider than the English and which the glossary warns against: `hu`, `eu`, `es`, `el`, `ro`, `sw`, `ta` (overlay and trailer line), `sl`, `pt-PT` (overlay only). "In-game purchases" instead of the store term: `cs`, `pl`, `sk`, `ru`, `sr-Cyrl`, `tr`, `ca` (`uk` uses the in-game form in every field). "Microtransactions": `fi`, `bg`. A shortened or alternative form of the store term: `ar`, `eo`, `fa`, `ms`, `bn`, `te`, `ur`, `id`. Other compromises to fit: a different word for ads on the overlay than everywhere else (`nl`, `it`, `sr-Cyrl`), a slash or ampersand (`id`, `ms`), singular nouns (`pt-BR`). The Italian draft had the bare-"purchases" widening and the reviewer restored "in-app"; the Malay draft said "app purchases" and was corrected.
- **One negation meant to cover two nouns.** The Persian overlay and trailer line could be read as "ad-free, and in-app purchases", the opposite of the claim; the reviewer rewrote both. The Vietnamese reviewer reports the same possible misreading still present in `shot_overlay_fair_deal` (no unambiguous wording fits 28 characters). The Swedish and German reviewers note a single "without" governing both nouns and judge that the normal, unambiguous reading.
- **"67 map generators" shortened on `shot_overlay_maps`.** Bare "generators" with "map" left implied: `de`, `pt-BR`, `pt-PT`, `fr`, `es`, `it`, `nl`, `ru`, `uk`, `fi`, `bg`, `te`. The Dutch, Finnish and Telugu reviewers note the bare word can also mean power or electrical generators. Replaced by "random landscapes" or "random kinds", so that generation is only implied: `el`, `sl`, `sw`, `ta`, `sv`. Label-style or coined wording: `ro`, `fa`, `th`, `bn` (mixes two words for "map").
- **Renderings of "premium currency".** Raised by the reviewer in 35 of 44 locales. Twenty use the gamer loan term or a calque of "premium currency" (`de`, `cs`, `da`, `pt-BR`, `hu`, `ca`, `fr`, `sk`, `sv`, `nl`, `el`, `ro`, `sr-Cyrl`, `nb`, `ms`, `ur`, `bg`, `hi`, `uk`, `fil`); fifteen use a descriptive "paid" or "purchasable currency" (`eo`, `eu`, `ar`, `fa`, `ru`, `sl`, `ko`, `zh-Hans`, `zh-Hant`, `ja`, `vi`, `sw`, `te`, `th`, `ta`). The shared question is whether a casual store visitor reads it as currency bought with real money, and not as "the game has no resources"; the glossary's fallback "no currency to buy" is available where the term proves unclear. The Persian draft's first rendering was open to a foreign-exchange reading and was changed. "Loot boxes" raises the same loanword-or-local-term question in `da`, `eo`, `eu`, `fr`, `ro`, `sl`, `sr-Cyrl`, `ko`, `ja`, `zh-Hans`, `zh-Hant`, `tr`, `fi`, `ms`.
- **"Computer opponent" kept apart from "AI agent".** Every reviewer who addresses it reports the two kept distinct. Points to check: (a) the short overlay form on `shot_overlay_opponents` can be parsed as "opponents of the computer" or "opponents on a computer" in `pt-BR`, `pt-PT`, `ca` and `eu`, is telegraphic in `fr`, less idiomatic in `ro`, and differs from the body term in `it` (fixed from the misparse); `es` uses "CPU", and the Japanese and Thai reviewers note players say "CPU" or "bot"; (b) "desktop players" in `multiplayer_crossplay` read as computer-controlled players and was fixed in `ca`, `pt-PT`, `sw`; (c) "play with a computer opponent" was ambiguous between with and against and was fixed in `tr`, `ur`, `hi`, `bn`; (d) the game's own interface calls its computer players "AI" in `pt-BR`, `el`, `zh-Hant`, `tr`, `sr-Cyrl` and `sl`, so the listing deliberately differs from the in-game menu there.
- **The word for "AI agent" and the co-play overlay.** The abbreviation is unsettled between a local form and Latin "AI" in `eu`, `hu`, `it`, `sl`, `nb`, `bg`; the noun is unsettled in `el`, `zh-Hant`, `vi`, `sw`. `sl`, `nb` and `th` use the Latin or English form in the hidden `windows_search_term_7` while the visible copy uses the local form. On `shot_overlay_ai_coplay` and `trailer_line_ai_coplay`, "your" is dropped in `pl`, `uk`, `hi`, `vi`; the line has no verb in `eu`, `el`; it is a statement instead of an imperative in `tr`, `fil`. The calque "play the same colony" was replaced in `sl`, `pt-PT`, `nb`, `ur`, `hi`, `bn`, `te`, `mr`.
- **"Colony" dropped from `ios_subtitle` and `shot_overlay_hero`.** The full genre term leaves no room in 30 and 28 characters, so the subtitle or hero overlay is plain "real-time strategy" without "colony" in `ar`, `bg`, `ca`, `cs`, `es`, `eu`, `fi`, `fil`, `fr`, `it`, `pt-BR`, `pt-PT`, `ro`, `ru`, `sk`, `sl`, `sr-Cyrl`, `tr`; `ta` drops it on the overlay only; `bn` keeps "colony" in the subtitle by dropping "real-time" and does the reverse on the overlay. "Colony" is not in `ios_keywords` either, because the field map's concept list excludes it (noted by `cs`, `fr`, `pt-PT`). Locales that kept it did so with a coined compound or an adapted phrase a marketer may want to revisit: `de`, `hu`, `nb`, `nl`, `da`, `sv`, `fa`, `pl`, `ur`, `th`.
- **Other cuts forced by short fields.** `play_short_description` drops "offline" (the field map's first cut) in `eu`, `ca`, `fr`, `es`, `it`, `ru`, `pt-PT`, `uk`, `fi`, `vi`; `pt-BR`, `el`, `sr-Cyrl`, `bg`, `bn` instead keep "offline" and shorten the colony-not-every-unit contrast. `short_pitch` and `desktop_short_pitch` omit the sentence "You set the priorities; your colony does the work." in `el` and `fi`. `appstream_summary` drops "rivals" in `ca`, `es`, `fi`. `trailer_line_3` is an adaptation or a noun phrase, not the English imperative, in `ro`, `sr-Cyrl`, `fi`, `bn`.
- **In-app purchase term differs between stores.** One shared field set serves every store, so where Apple and Google use different local labels the Apple-facing text (including `short_pitch` as App Store promotional text) does not carry Apple's exact term, or the reverse: `pt-BR`, `ca`, `es`, `sv`, `zh-Hant`, `zh-Hans`, `ru`, `ko`, `ja`, `pt-PT`. The store label could not be verified in `uk`, `ur`, `fil`, `fi` and `th`. `bn` was changed to Google Play's live term.
- **Right-to-left embedding and script rendering.** The Urdu reviewer rewrote eight fields so they open with an Urdu word, because a field that begins with a Latin-script term can flip paragraph direction where a store auto-detects it; `epic_legal`, `trailer_title` and `itch_downloads_note` still begin with a Latin name in `ur`, and `epic_legal` does in `fa` and `ar`. The Arabic reviewer asks for a platform preview of every field with embedded Latin terms (MCP, JavaScript, iPhone/iPad, URLs). For the caption and trailer renderers: Thai has no word spaces and the catalog contains no zero-width spaces, so line breaking must be Thai-aware; `bn` now uses Bengali digits and `fa` Persian digits, so fonts must cover them; Devanagari conjuncts in `hi` can render wider than the character count suggests. `fr` uses a no-break space (U+00A0) before colons and semicolons, which a console may normalise and show as a whitespace-only difference on readback.
- **Apple silicon wording in `mac_tail`.** Changed to follow Apple's local spelling in `hu` and `sk`, and reworded to store phrasing in `nb`. Not verified against Apple's current local pages in `cs`, `da`, `ar`, `el`, `uk`, `th`; capitalisation ("Apple silicon" or "Apple Silicon") and the chip-or-processor noun vary between catalogs. `es` avoids the regional un Mac / una Mac split by omitting the article.
- **Keyword choices are not backed by search-volume data.** No reviewer had App Store or Microsoft Store search data; reviewers in about 40 of the 44 locales say explicitly that `ios_keywords` and the `windows_search_term_*` fields are judgement. Recurring moves: adding the local "without internet" (or "wifi") phrase as the search form of "offline", which some reviewers note is not literally on the field map's concept list (`cs`, `sr-Cyrl`, `nl`, `zh-Hans`, `zh-Hant`, `te`); dropping LAN, map editor, economy, tactics or skirmish for space; hidden search terms deliberately spelled as people type rather than as the visible copy spells them (`sl`, `nb`, `tr`, `hi`, `fa`, `fi`, `th`). Reviewers for `eo`, `eu`, `sr-Cyrl`, `ur`, `mr`, `hi`, `ta`, `bn`, `sw`, `fil` and `th` note that players may search in English, in Latin script or in another language, or that the store has no localisation for the language. How Apple tokenises or folds the script is unverified for `ar`, `vi`, `th` and `el`; compound subtitles in `nl`, `hu` and `nb` will not index their component words.
- **The game's own terms against plainer words.** The glossary requires in-game names, and several read oddly in store copy: building names in `de`, `cs`, `ru`, `tr`, `ca`, `sl`, `ms`, `bg`, `mr`, `pl`, `bn`; the word for the 67 landscapes in `tr`, `ur`, `te`, `fil`, `mr`. The creature name "globs" differs from the in-game name in `cs`, `pl`, `nl`, `ko`, and has an awkward homonym or near-homonym in `sr-Cyrl`, `sl`, `pl`, `fa`, `ta`; `ur` was switched to the in-game name. The word for "colony" differs from the in-game word in `fa`, `hi`, `sw`, and the in-game word carries a colonial-history or other everyday connotation in `ko`, `vi`, `bn`, `ta`. "Casual" follows the game interface but can read as "with friends" in `hu`, `eu`, `pl`, `fr` (fixed for that reason in `cs`), and players often use the English loanwords for ranked and casual (`da`, `nl`, `sv`, `sr-Cyrl`, `el`, `ms`, `ur`). "Quick match" contains a speed word the glossary cautions about in `de`, `da`, `nl`, `ko`, `ur`, `mr`.
- **`epic_tail` and `epic_legal` against the text saved in the Epic console.** The field map marks both as unchanged from storefront-v1, and most reviewers left them byte-identical. Three catalogs now differ from v1: `zh-Hans` (a carried-over v1 meaning error, "the player must create an account", corrected, and the guest term aligned), `sv` (the translator's grammatical corrections kept) and `bn` (one term in `epic_legal`). If the v1 text is saved in the Epic console for those locales, it differs from the catalog. Small inconsistencies between the retained v1 legal text and the new v2 fields were noted and left in `ro`, `ru`, `sr-Cyrl`, `id`, `zh-Hant`, `mr`, `tr`, `ko`, `vi`, `ur`.
- **Register and regional choices.** Formal address is used throughout in `cs`, `el`, `bg`, `tr`, `sl`; the `cs`, `el`, `bg` and `tr` reviewers note that some mobile listings use the informal form; `id` uses informal address where the game uses formal. `es` pairs a Latin American and a European Spanish word in `ai_body` while being uploaded to es-ES slots.
- **Fields sitting exactly at their limit.** Many overlays (28), trailer lines (32), captions (100), `play_short_description` (80) and `ios_keywords` (100 bytes) are at or within a character or two of the limit; reviewers for `ar`, `eo`, `eu`, `fa`, `fil`, `fr`, `hu`, `id`, `mr`, `ms`, `nl`, `pl`, `ta`, `te`, `th`, `vi` ask that any later edit be recounted, and `nb` and `hi` ask that the rendered images be checked for clipping.
- **Error classes the reviewers did fix, as a guide to what may remain elsewhere.** "Play 23 maps" reading as playing cards (`nl`, `bg`) or as maps being the thing played (`ur`, `sw`); "67 random landscapes" reading as "67 random countries" (`sk`) or "67 uneven terrains" (`ta`); "where rival colonies meet" reading as "where they are located" (`ca`, `pt-PT`, `ur`); a modifier attached to the wrong noun in `feature_scripting` (`es`, `pt-BR`, `eu`); added promises or intensifiers (`ja`, `sl`, `hu`, `pl`, `ko`); idioms and homographs with an unintended meaning (`sl`, `fa`, `tr`, `ur`, `ko`).

## Totals

- **Ratings.** 7 of 44 locales rated publishable (`de`, `es`, `fr`, `id`, `ja`, `sv`, `zh-Hant`); 37 of 44 rated publishable with concerns. No locale was rated lower.
- **Serious errors fixed.** 171 entries in 30 locales; 14 locales reported none (`ar`, `da`, `de`, `el`, `fi`, `fr`, `id`, `mr`, `ro`, `sv`, `te`, `th`, `uk`, `vi`). In 21 of those 30 locales the reviewer added a statement that no error, or no further error, of the classes named in the brief was found; see "How to read the counts" above.
- **Fields changed by reviewers.** 569 field edits across all 44 locales (of 78 fields each); every reviewer changed at least 3 and at most 33.
- **Residual concerns.** 517 entries across all 44 locales.

## Per-locale results

### ar — Arabic

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ar.md](../translations/ar.md). Review record: [reviews/ar.json](../translations/reviews/ar.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (9): `headline`, `short_pitch`, `ios_keywords`, `control_body`, `defense_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- shot_overlay_ai_coplay / trailer_line_ai_coplay: 'العب مع وكيل ذكائك الاصطناعي' is grammatical (possessive on the second term of the compound, like 'جواز سفرك') and exactly 28 characters, but a native speaker should confirm it reads naturally. 'العب بجانب وكيل ذكاء اصطناعي' also fits 28 but loses 'your'.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal / trailer_line_4 use the short form 'مشتريات داخلية' because the store term 'عمليات شراء داخل التطبيق' cannot fit in 28 characters with 'no ads'. A native speaker should confirm players read it as in-app purchases. The body fields use the full store term.
- 'premium currency' is rendered 'عملات مدفوعة' (paid currencies) in fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal. I believe this is the usual gamer wording, but the glossary fallback 'بدون عملات تُشترى' (no currency to buy) is available if a native reader finds it unclear.
- 'globs' is 'الكريات', the name the game's own Arabic catalog (texts.ar.txt) uses. It is a translation ('globules') rather than a transliteration; I kept it for consistency with the in-game text.
- 'النُزل' is used as the plural of inn, following the game's Arabic catalog; the dictionary plural is 'أنزال'. A native speaker may want to confirm.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'استراتيجية في الوقت الحقيقي' (27 characters): 'colony' is dropped because it cannot fit with the full genre term.
- ios_keywords (94 of 100 bytes) is now split into single words so the App Store can combine them ('بدون' + 'إعلانات' / 'نت'). It uses standard hamza spelling ('إعلانات'); many users type 'اعلانات', and I could not verify whether App Store search normalises alef forms. Economy, tactics and map editor are left out for space.
- mac_tail keeps 'Apple silicon' in Latin letters. Apple's Arabic pages may write 'سيليكون Apple'; I could not verify this, so check against Apple's current Arabic wording.
- shot_caption_fair_deal is exactly at the 100-character limit and the two overlay fields above are exactly at 28; any later edit must recount.
- 'لعبة محلية' / 'الألعاب المحلية' (local game) follows the game's own Arabic string, but out of context it could be read as 'locally made game' in shot_caption_ai_coplay.
- epic_legal (unchanged v1 text) starts with the Latin product name, so in auto-direction contexts the paragraph may render left-to-right; RTL rendering of all fields with embedded Latin terms (MCP, JavaScript, iPhone/iPad, URLs) still needs a platform preview check.
- multiplayer_heading uses the plural imperative 'العبوا معًا' while the rest of the listing addresses a single player; this is natural for 'play together' but is a deliberate register exception.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; my nine edits are clarity and naturalness fixes. All 78 fields were back-translated against en.md, but this is AI review, not native-speaker validation.

### bg — Bulgarian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [bg.md](../translations/bg.md). Review record: [reviews/bg.json](../translations/reviews/bg.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (14): `short_pitch`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `play_short_description`, `ios_keywords`, `map_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `itch_downloads_note`, `shot_overlay_territory`, `shot_caption_build`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (8)**

- map_body: 'Играйте на 23 ръчно изработени карти' echoes the idiom 'играя на карти' (play cards); reworded to 'Играйте на някоя от 23-те ръчно изработени карти' so it reads only as maps
- shot_overlay_territory: 'Изберете накъде да растете' read as 'choose which way you grow' and did not match the expansion wording elsewhere; now 'Решете къде да се разширите' (27 characters)
- android_tail / ios_tail / mac_tail: 'Играта за един играч работи офлайн' could read as 'the game, which is for one player'; now 'Режимът за един играч работи офлайн'
- open_source_body: 'Globulation 2 започна през 2001 г.' was unidiomatic and blurred the two facts; now 'Работата по Globulation 2 започна през 2001 г., а през 2004 г. играта стана публично достъпна'
- itch_downloads_note: 'В itch.io MCP сървърът' ran two Latin names together so 'itch.io MCP' looked like one name; sentence restructured
- play_short_description: bare 'не единици' was clipped; now 'не единиците' (exactly 80 characters, keeps genre, no ads, the contrast and offline)
- ios_keywords: replaced 'тактика' and 'LAN' with 'интернет' so that the common Bulgarian offline query 'без интернет' is matched together with the existing 'без' (97 of 100 bytes)
- ai_body: dropped a redundant pronoun ('какво прави той'); multiplayer_online_body: 'влезте с акаунт' changed to 'влезте в акаунта си' and future tense for the computer-opponent fallback; short_pitch / desktop_short_pitch: added the conjunction 'а' for a natural contrast; shot_caption_build: imperative to match the English and the other captions

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 use 'микроплащания' (microtransactions) for in-app purchases because the store term 'покупки в приложението' cannot fit in 28/32 characters; a native speaker should confirm this is the best short equivalent ('микротранзакции' is one character too long for the overlay).
- 'премиум валута' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal) is the gamer loan term for premium currency; a native speaker should confirm it is clear to casual players and does not need 'валута за купуване'.
- 'ИИ агент' is used throughout for AI agent (matching the game's own 'ИИ'); Bulgarian tech press also writes 'AI агент'. A native speaker may prefer the Latin form, especially in windows_search_term_7.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and shortens the contrast to 'не единиците' in order to keep 'Офлайн'; the field map's strict cut order would drop 'offline' first. Alternative at 79 characters: 'Стратегия в реално време без реклами. Командвате колонията, а не всяка единица.'
- ios_keywords: 'интернет' is included only to form the offline query 'без интернет'; LAN, tactics, multiplayer, economy and map editor are left out for space. Real App Store search volumes for Bulgarian were not checked.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Стратегия в реално време' with no 'colony', because a version with 'колония' does not fit in 30/28 characters.
- shot_overlay_maps ' 23 карти и 67 генератора' relies on the picture for 'generators' to be read as map generators; 'генератора на карти' does not fit in 28.
- 'Хановете' (inns, the game's own term 'хан') is a homograph of 'the khans'; context makes it clear, but it is worth a native glance in economy_body and defense_body.
- Ranked/casual is rendered 'класирани или обикновени'; the game UI says 'Класиран' and 'Без рейтинг'. 'Обикновени' was kept for readability.
- Polite plural (вие) register is used throughout; a native games marketer may prefer informal singular (ти) for a game listing. This is a style choice, applied consistently.
- The whole catalog is an AI translation with AI review; no native human speaker has validated it.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the 'maps as playing cards' class, false claims, forbidden price words, wrong platform mentions, protected-term damage or broken limits were found; the catalog I received was already a storefront-v2 translation (not v1 as the task text said), structurally clean and using the game's own Bulgarian terms (глобули, хан, рояк, хиподрум, пейзаж, повторения, класиран). The items below are the most material fixes, none of them a serious error.

### bn — Bengali

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [bn.md](../translations/bn.md). Review record: [reviews/bn.json](../translations/reviews/bn.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (31): `headline`, `fair_deal_line`, `short_pitch`, `play_short_description`, `ios_subtitle`, `ios_keywords`, `appstream_summary`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `windows_search_term_1`, `windows_search_term_3`, `listing_intro`, `map_heading`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_maps`, `feature_opponents`, `feature_colonies`, `feature_tutorial`, `feature_offline`, `feature_languages`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_heading`, `open_source_body`, `amazon_tail`, `epic_legal`, `shot_overlay_hero`, `shot_overlay_maps`, `shot_overlay_opponents`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (12)**

- headline / short_pitch / play_short_description / ios_subtitle / appstream_summary / desktop_short_pitch / listing_intro / windows_search_term_1 / shot_overlay_hero: the genre term was 'রিয়েল-টাইম কৌশল'. Google Play's own Bengali category label is 'স্ট্র্যাটেজি' (checked on the live hl=bn category page) and that is what players type, so the genre term is now 'রিয়েল-টাইম স্ট্র্যাটেজি' everywhere. The longer word forced re-adaptation of the tight fields (see residual concerns).
- fair_deal_line / short_pitch / desktop_short_pitch / shot_caption_fair_deal: 'in-app purchases' was 'ইন-অ্যাপ কেনাকাটা'. The glossary requires the store's exact term; Google Play's live Bengali pages use 'অ্যাপ-মধ্যস্থ কেনাকাটা' (checked on the Play Pass page), so the body fields now use it.
- shot_overlay_maps: '67 দৈব ভূদৃশ্য' did not say the maps are generated, and a bare 'দৈব' reads first as 'divine/providential'. Replaced with '২৩ ম্যাপ, ৬৭ ম্যাপ জেনারেটর', which mirrors the English.
- map_heading / map_body: 'কোথায় বিস্তার করবেন' used a transitive verb with no object (ungrammatical ellipsis). Now 'উপনিবেশ কোন দিকে বাড়াবেন'. 'worth fighting for' was rendered 'should be fought for' (উচিত); now 'সার্থক'.
- defense_body: 'খাবার আর হাসপাতালের জোগান' read as 'a supply of hospitals'. Rewritten so the attack lasts only while food and hospitals support it.
- multiplayer_online_body: 'কম্পিউটার প্রতিপক্ষের সাথে খেলবেন' (play with a computer opponent) changed to 'বিরুদ্ধে' (against), so it cannot be read as playing on the same side, which is the AI-agent wording.
- open_source_heading / open_source_body / epic_legal: the hybrid 'উন্মুক্ত সোর্স' replaced by the standard 'ওপেন সোর্স', which windows_search_term_6 already used. In open_source_body the sentence also read as if the zero price were 'under the GPL'; price and licence are now separate clauses.
- All counts and years (23, 67, 8, 16, 10, 45, 2001, 2004, GPL version 3) were Latin digits glued to Bengali suffixes ('23টি'). Converted to Bengali numerals, which Google's own Bengali store pages use; the checker accepts them. 'Globulation 2' and '1v1' keep Latin digits.
- ai_body / feature_ai_coplay: 'একই উপনিবেশ খেলুন' (calque of 'play the same colony') changed to 'একই উপনিবেশ নিয়ে খেলুন'; 'নিজের এআই এজেন্ট আপনিই নিয়ে আসবেন' changed to 'নিজের এআই এজেন্টের ব্যবস্থা আপনিই করবেন'; two consecutive 'এটি' with different referents disambiguated.
- ios_keywords: 'সেনা' swapped for 'কৌশল' so the native-word spelling of strategy/tactics is searchable now that the subtitle uses the loanword. 96 of 100 bytes, no word repeated from the name or subtitle.
- windows_search_term_3: literal 'ঘাঁটি নির্মাণ' replaced by the loanword players use, 'বেস বিল্ডিং'.
- amazon_tail: 'অনলাইনে একাধিক খেলোয়াড়ের খেলা নেই' replaced by the standard 'অনলাইন মাল্টিপ্লেয়ার নেই'.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (14)**

- No native speaker has read this. Review was AI-only; store terms were checked against live Google Play Bengali pages, Apple's Bengali terms could not be checked.
- In-app purchases appears in two forms. Body fields use Google Play's 'অ্যাপ-মধ্যস্থ কেনাকাটা'. shot_overlay_fair_deal (28/28) and trailer_line_4 (32/32) use the shorter 'ইন-অ্যাপ কেনা নেই' because the store term does not fit. A native speaker should confirm 'ইন-অ্যাপ কেনা' reads as in-app purchases, and whether one form throughout would be better.
- ios_subtitle is 'উপনিবেশ গড়ার স্ট্র্যাটেজি গেম' (colony-building strategy game), exactly 30/30. 'Real-time' was dropped per the field map's cut order, and 'গড়ার' (building) is not in the English subtitle, though it matches 'grow a colony' in the pitch. shot_overlay_hero carries 'রিয়েল-টাইম স্ট্র্যাটেজি' and drops 'colony', so the two are no longer identical as they are in English.
- shot_overlay_maps uses 'ম্যাপ' while every other field uses the game's own word 'মানচিত্র'; 'মানচিত্র' with 'ম্যাপ জেনারেটর' is 30 characters, over the 28 limit. A native speaker should confirm the mix is acceptable.
- play_short_description shortens 'not every unit' to 'ইউনিট নয়, গোটা উপনিবেশ চালান' (not units, the whole colony) so that 'offline' and 'no ads' both stay within 80 (77 used).
- short_pitch (169/170) drops 'আপনার' from 'your colony does the work' and uses 'গড়ুন' instead of 'গড়ে তুলুন'; desktop_short_pitch (267/270) keeps the fuller wording. appstream_summary (77/78) uses singular 'প্রতিদ্বন্দ্বীর' (rival, generic) to fit.
- epic_legal was marked 'unchanged, keep the v1 translation' in the field map. I changed one term in it ('উন্মুক্ত সোর্সের' to 'ওপেন সোর্স') for consistency with the open-source section; revert that if the v1 text must stay byte-identical. epic_tail is untouched.
- Bengali numerals are now used in overlay captions and feature bullets (২৩, ৬৭, ৮, ১৬, ১০, ৪৫, ২০০১, ২০০৪, ৩). The screenshot and trailer renderers need a font with Bengali digits. If the project wants Latin digits in every locale, this is a mechanical swap back.
- 'স্থানীয় খেলা' for 'local game' (ai_body, shot_caption_ai_coplay) is literal and could be taken for LAN play or even local sport; gamers may say 'লোকাল গেম'. Kept because the English has the same ambiguity.
- The game's own words 'দৈবভাবে' (randomly) and 'মৌচাক' (swarm, literally beehive) are kept in body text to match the in-game UI; 'দৈব' can also read as 'by providence'. The game menu calls the tutorial 'প্রশিক্ষণ' while the listing says 'টিউটোরিয়াল', to avoid confusion with unit training.
- 'উপনিবেশ' (the game's word for colony) carries a colonial-history connotation in Bengali; kept for consistency with the game UI.
- ios_keywords are Bengali-script terms plus RTS and LAN. Many Bengali-speaking users search the App Store in English, so real search value is unverified.
- trailer_line_3 is a noun phrase ('প্রতিদ্বন্দ্বীর সাথে জমির লড়াই।', 32/32), not an imperative as in English; the imperative does not fit in 32.
- 'দুই দশক ধরে' (over two decades) in open_source_body can suggest continuous development, as the English does.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning-reversing error, false claim, forbidden price word, missing protected term or broken limit was found in the translator's draft; it passed the checker before I touched it. The items below are the substantive terminology and clarity defects I fixed.

### ca — Catalan

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ca.md](../translations/ca.md). Review record: [reviews/ca.json](../translations/reviews/ca.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (22): `short_pitch`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `appstream_summary`, `listing_intro`, `map_body`, `defense_heading`, `defense_body`, `feature_replays`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_lan_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `itch_downloads_note`, `shot_overlay_battle`, `shot_caption_battle`, `shot_caption_territory`, `shot_caption_maps`, `shot_caption_opponents`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`, `trailer_line_3`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (6)**

- shot_caption_territory: 'on es troben les colònies rivals' reads first as 'where the rival colonies are located', not 'where they meet'; now 'on entren en contacte les colònies rivals'
- multiplayer_crossplay: 'Els jugadors d'ordinador' can be read as 'computer players' (the built-in opponents) instead of people playing on desktop; now 'Qui juga des de l'ordinador, el navegador o el mòbil'
- map_body: 'per què val la pena lluitar' reads as 'why it is worth fighting' rather than 'what is worth fighting for'; now 'per quines coses val la pena lluitar'
- ai_body: told players to turn the MCP server on in 'Opcions', but the game's Catalan main-menu button ([settings]) is 'Configuració'; corrected. Also 'només funciona al teu propi ordinador' (works only on) changed to 's'executa' (runs only on)
- shot_caption_ai_coplay: 'el propi agent d'IA del jugador' used 'propi' in the non-standard 'itself' position; now 'l'agent d'IA propi del jugador' so it clearly means the player's own agent
- open_source_body: bare preverbal subject 'Voluntaris l'han anat construint' was ungrammatical-sounding; now 'L'han anat construint voluntaris'. 'sota la llicència GPL' (calque) changed to 'amb llicència GPL'

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- 'land' is now 'territori' everywhere it is fought over (was 'la terra', which can read as the Earth/soil/homeland); 'explorar terres noves' kept in control_body. A native speaker may prefer 'terra' for the plainer tone.
- To fit 'territori': appstream_summary drops 'rivals' ('fes créixer una colònia i lluita pel territori', 72/78), and short_pitch/desktop_short_pitch use 'Tu fixes les prioritats' instead of 'Tu marques les prioritats'. desktop_short_pitch is exactly 270/270 and short_pitch 169/170; trailer_line_3 is exactly 32/32.
- In-app purchases: kept 'compres integrades', which a web check showed is the Catalan App Store label ('Ofereix compres integrades'). Google Play's Catalan term is 'compres des de l'aplicació'; it does not fit the short fields, so one term is used throughout. Overlay and trailer line 4 use the shorter 'compres al joc' ('Ni anuncis ni compres al joc', 28/28).
- shot_overlay_opponents '8 oponents de l'ordinador' (25/28): natural reading is 'computer opponents' but 'de l'ordinador' could in theory be read as 'opponents of the computer'. The full form 'oponents controlats per l'ordinador' does not fit 28. Alternative that fits: 'Contra l'ordinador: 8 rivals'.
- play_short_description omits 'offline' (per the field map's cut order) because the full genre term plus 'sense anuncis' plus the colony/unit contrast uses 76/80.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Estratègia en temps real' without 'colony' (adding it exceeds 30/28).
- 'moneda prèmium' and 'caixes de botí' are the terms used in Catalan gaming press; a native should confirm 'moneda prèmium' is clearer to players than 'moneda de pagament'.
- 'Mantén la colònia alimentada' (heading, overlay 28/28, trailer) is correct but slightly literal; 'Que la colònia no passi gana' (28) is a more idiomatic alternative a native marketer might prefer.
- shot_overlay_build/trailer_line_1 use 'Tu manes. Ells construeixen' ('you give the orders') because 'Tu decideixes. Ells construeixen' is 31 characters; control_heading keeps 'Tu decideixes'.
- Building names follow the game's Catalan strings, including 'granges' for swarms and 'poliesportius' for racetracks, and 'despenses' for inns; these read oddly out of context ('Les granges en produeixen més') but match what players see in game. Defense tower is 'Torre' in game; listing says 'torres de defensa'.
- ios_keywords (exactly 100 bytes) left as the translator chose; actual Catalan App Store search behaviour ('sense connexió' vs 'sense internet', 'construcció' vs 'construir') was not verifiable.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are the unchanged reviewed v1 text (verified identical to the committed version); not re-edited.

### cs — Czech

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [cs.md](../translations/cs.md). Review record: [reviews/cs.json](../translations/reviews/cs.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (14): `short_pitch`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `ios_keywords`, `listing_intro`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_languages`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `shot_caption_build`, `shot_caption_maps`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (2)**

- multiplayer_online_body: 'casual' was rendered as 'přátelské' (friendly matches), which in Czech reads as matches between friends and blurs into the invite-a-friend room feature; changed to 'nehodnocené', the term the game's own Czech interface uses opposite 'Hodnocené'.
- shot_caption_ai_coplay: 'hráčovým vlastním AI agentem, připojeným' was a stilted calque; rewritten as 'Místní hra, kterou hráč sdílí s vlastním AI agentem připojeným přes vestavěný server MCP.' (89/100). Fluency fix, meaning unchanged.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (10)**

- ios_keywords: I dropped 'bitva' and added 'bez internetu' as the native search form of the 'offline' concept (alongside 'offline'); now exactly 100 of 100 UTF-8 bytes. A native speaker or ASO check should confirm 'bez internetu' is acceptable under the concepts-only rule and that noun forms (válka, armáda, taktika) outperform adjective forms (válečná).
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Strategie v reálném čase' (24): 'colony' does not fit within 30/28 with the full genre term, so the Czech subtitle does not carry 'kolonie', and it is not in the keywords either (not on the allowed concept list).
- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 use 'nákupů ve hře' (the PEGI-style 'in-game purchases' term) because 'nákupů v aplikaci' exceeds 28/32 characters; fair_deal_line, the pitches and shot_caption_fair_deal use the store term 'nákupy v aplikaci'. The two terms are both standard but differ.
- 'globs' is rendered 'globové' (gen. 'globů'), consistently in the description body only. The game's own Czech tutorial calls the creatures 'globulové' (for English 'globules'); a native speaker may prefer aligning the listing with that.
- 'Uživte svou kolonii' for 'Keep the colony fed' (heading, overlay, trailer line 2): natural, but 'uživit' also carries a 'provide a livelihood' nuance; a native marketer might prefer 'Nenechte kolonii hladovět'.
- 'prémiová měna' and 'lootboxy' are the usual Czech gamer terms; a native check that 'prémiová měna' is immediately understood as paid currency by casual store visitors would be worthwhile.
- Register is formal plural (vykání) throughout; some Czech mobile game listings use informal address (tykání). Consistent, but a style choice for a native marketer to confirm.
- 'train' is now 'trénovat' in listing_intro, economy_body and defense_body (to avoid the 'cvičiště je cvičí' echo), while the game's interface uses 'cvičit/výcvik'; both are natural.
- mac_tail 'Mac s čipem Apple silicon': Apple's own Czech phrasing varies; confirm against the current Czech App Store wording.
- In defense_body the line 'válka se vyhrává v hospodách stejně jako na frontě' keeps the English figure of speech; 'hospoda' is the game's own name for the Inn but also means 'pub', which gives the line a mildly humorous ring in Czech.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the maps/cards, tabletop, free-as-freedom or computer-opponent/AI-agent class were found; no forbidden price word, no other platform in Apple-facing fields, all protected terms and numbers intact, all limits respected by count.

### el — Greek

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [el.md](../translations/el.md). Review record: [reviews/el.json](../translations/reviews/el.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (9): `headline`, `feature_control`, `ios_keywords`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `ios_tail`, `shot_caption_battle`, `shot_caption_maps`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (14)**

- The task's validation command cannot see composed store-field limits for el (those errors are prefixed with the store id, and the checker skips them while any other locale has errors). I measured them separately for el: Google Play, F-Droid/fastlane, App Store, Mac App Store and Microsoft all compose within limits (Play short description 77/80, Apple promotional text 149/170, Microsoft short description 259/270).
- ios_keywords (now 'offline,χωρίς,διαφημίσεις,ίντερνετ,πόλεμος,χτίσιμο,multiplayer', exactly 100 bytes): I replaced 'βάσης' and 'LAN' with 'ίντερνετ' so that 'χωρίς ίντερνετ', the common Greek way to search for offline games, is covered. This rests on my judgement of Greek search habits, not on search-volume data; someone with App Store keyword data for Greece should confirm. Greek users often search in the genitive ('παιχνίδια στρατηγικής', 'πολέμου'), and I do not know whether Apple matches inflected Greek forms.
- 'πράκτορας AI' for 'AI agent' is the usual Greek tech-press term, but some readers know only the English 'AI agent' and Microsoft's Greek localisation uses 'εκπρόσωπος'. A native speaker should confirm it reads naturally in ai_heading, the overlay and the trailer line.
- 'αντίπαλοι υπολογιστή' for 'computer opponents' is compact and clearly distinct from 'πράκτορας AI', but the game's own Greek UI says 'Αντίπαλοι ΤΝ'. The listing avoids 'ΤΝ'/'AI' on purpose, per the glossary. In feature_scripting, 'τον δικό σας αντίπαλο υπολογιστή' can also be parsed as 'your own rival computer'; the meaning survives, but a native check is worthwhile.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal ('Χωρίς διαφημίσεις ή αγορές') and trailer_line_4 ('Χωρίς διαφημίσεις. Χωρίς αγορές.') shorten 'in-app purchases' to 'αγορές' because the full store term 'αγορές εντός εφαρμογής' cannot fit in 28/32 characters next to 'no ads'. This is slightly broader than the English; all longer fields use the full term.
- 'premium νόμισμα' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal) is the loanword Greek gaming press uses. A non-gamer may find it unclear; the alternative would be 'νόμισμα που αγοράζεται με πραγματικά χρήματα'.
- Genre term: headline, desktop_short_pitch and listing_intro use the full 'στρατηγική σε πραγματικό χρόνο'; ios_subtitle, shot_overlay_hero, play_short_description and appstream_summary use 'Στρατηγική RTS' because the full term (30 characters) does not fit. The glossary asks for one term across headline, subtitle, hero overlay and Play short description; this is the same genre in long and acronym form, not literally the same wording.
- short_pitch and desktop_short_pitch drop the sentence 'You set the priorities; your colony does the work.' This is the first cut the field map allows; Greek cannot fit it in 170/270.
- play_short_description keeps 'Offline' and shortens the contrast to 'Διοικείτε μια αποικία, όχι μονάδες' (77/80). The full 'όχι κάθε μονάδα' only fits if 'Offline' is dropped.
- shot_overlay_maps says '67 τυχαία τοπία' (the game's own word 'Τοπίο' for landscape). 'Map generators' ('γεννήτριες χαρτών') does not fit in 28 characters, so the caption relies on 'τυχαία' to signal generated maps.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay and trailer_line_ai_coplay ('Μαζί με τον πράκτορα AI σας') have no verb: 'Παίξτε με…' exceeds 28 characters and can read as 'play against' in Greek. A native marketer may prefer another solution.
- Online terms follow the game's Greek UI ('Βαθμολογούμενος', 'Χαλαρός', 'Γρήγορος αγώνας', 'Αίθουσα'). Many Greek players say 'αγώνες κατάταξης' or 'ranked' and 'δωμάτιο'; I kept the UI wording so the listing matches what players see in the game.
- Register is polite plural (εσείς), matching the game's Greek tutorial text. Some Greek mobile-game listings use informal singular; this is a style choice a native marketer may want to revisit.
- mac_tail keeps 'Apple silicon' in Latin as a platform name; I did not verify Apple's current Greek wording. epic_tail and epic_legal are the unchanged, previously reviewed v1 text, as the field map requires.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found; the nine edits are naturalness and clarity fixes. The catalog I reviewed was already the storefront-v2 translation, not the v1 text the task described.

### eo — Esperanto

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [eo.md](../translations/eo.md). Review record: [reviews/eo.json](../translations/reviews/eo.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (6): `ios_keywords`, `windows_search_term_6`, `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `shot_caption_opponents`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (5)**

- shot_caption_opponents: (minor) 'dividas unu mapon' could be read as 'split up one map'; changed to 'kunhavas unu mapon' (share)
- multiplayer_online_body: (minor) 'gastigu ĉambron' clashed with 'kiel gasto' in the same field and did not match the game UI; changed to 'kreu ĉambron', the game's own button label ('Krei ĉambron')
- map_body / feature_maps: (minor) clumsy 'el iu ajn el la 67 ... pejzaĝoj'; now 'laŭ iu ajn el la 67 hazardaj pejzaĝoj' and 'generataj laŭ 67 hazardaj pejzaĝoj'
- ios_keywords: (minor) literal compounds 'bazkonstruado' and 'mapredaktilo' replaced with single words a searcher would type; now 'RTS,bazo,konstruado,senreta,sen,reklamoj,milito,armeo,batalo,plurludanta,LAN,mapo,ekonomio,taktiko' (98 bytes, adds the army concept, drops 'editor')
- windows_search_term_6: (minor) adjective 'malfermkoda' replaced with the noun phrase 'malfermita kodo' as a search phrase

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- Esperanto has no official App Store / Google Play localisation, so there is no store-standard term for 'in-app purchases'. The catalog uses 'enaplikaĵaj aĉetoj' in running text and the shortened 'enaj aĉetoj' in shot_overlay_fair_deal (28/28) and trailer_line_4, because 'enludaj' or 'enaplikaĵaj' do not fit 28/32 characters. A native speaker should confirm 'enaj aĉetoj' reads clearly as in-app purchases on its own.
- 'loot boxes' is rendered 'hazardskatoloj' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal). This is a coined, transparent compound, not an attested community term; 'hazardkestoj', 'predkestoj' or the English loanword are alternatives a native speaker may prefer.
- 'premium currency' is rendered 'aĉetebla ludmono' (purchasable game money), following the glossary's 'no currency to buy' fallback; worth a native check.
- shot_caption_economy uses 'globulejoj', the game's own Esperanto name for the swarm building. It contains the root of 'globuloj' (globs) in a standalone caption; kept because the glossary requires the in-game building name.
- 'ranked/casual/quick match' use the game's own UI terms ('rangigita', 'senstreĉa', 'rapida matĉo'); 'senstreĉa' literally means 'stress-free'. It matches what players see in the game, but it is an unusual word for 'casual'.
- multiplayer_crossplay renders 'mobile' as 'portebla aparato' (portable device), which could loosely include laptops; 'poŝaparato' or 'telefono aŭ tabulkomputilo' are alternatives.
- 'tero' is used for 'land' throughout (e.g. 'Batalu pri la tero'); lowercase it means land/ground, but a reader could briefly think of the planet. 'teritorio' is the unambiguous alternative and would still fit the limits.
- short_pitch is at 169/170 and trailer_line_3 at 32/32: within limits but with no slack for later edits.
- ios_keywords and windows_search_term_\* are best guesses: there is no Esperanto App Store or Microsoft Store storefront or search-volume data to confirm what Esperanto speakers type.
- The website's Esperanto copy says 'malfermfonteca' for open source while the store catalog says 'malfermkoda' / 'malfermita kodo'; both are in use, but the project may want one term across properties.
- No human or native-speaker validation; this is AI translation with independent AI review.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- none: no meaning errors, false claims, price-word violations, cross-platform mentions in Apple-facing fields, broken protected terms or exceeded limits were found in the translator's catalog; all six edits are improvements to wording or search terms, plus a preamble wording update (not a field)

### es — Spanish

Rating: **Publishable**. Catalog: [es.md](../translations/es.md). Review record: [reviews/es.json](../translations/reviews/es.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (11): `ios_keywords`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_maps`, `feature_replays`, `feature_scripting`, `open_source_heading`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (2)**

- shot_caption_fair_deal: 'El juego recién instalado' (the game 'freshly installed') shifted 'the game as installed' towards 'right after install', hinting the deal could change later; reworded to 'El juego que instalas' (96/100 characters)
- feature_scripting: 'Programa tu propio rival controlado por la CPU en JavaScript' attached 'en JavaScript' to the CPU rather than to the writing; reordered to 'Programa en JavaScript tu propio rival controlado por la CPU'

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal / trailer_line_4 use 'Sin anuncios ni compras' (23 characters): the full store term 'compras dentro de la app' cannot fit in 28 characters, so 'in-app' is implied by context only. A native speaker should confirm this is preferable to 'Sin anuncios ni micropagos' (26), and that it does not read as a price claim.
- In-app purchases is rendered with Apple's term 'compras dentro de la app' everywhere; Google Play es-ES itself prints 'compras en la aplicación'. Both are understood; one wording was kept for consistency.
- Computer opponent is 'rival controlado por la CPU' (overlay: 'Enfréntate a 8 rivales CPU'). It is clearly distinct from 'agente de IA' and is gamer-standard, but casual mobile players may find 'CPU' less transparent than 'la máquina'; worth a native check.
- Regional neutrality in ai_body: 'computadora' (Latin American) is paired with 'ratón' (Spain; also what the game UI uses). This is the Wikipedia-style neutral compromise, but the catalog is uploaded to es-ES store slots, where 'ordenador' would be the local word.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 mapas y 67 generadores' relies on ellipsis for 'generadores de mapas' (the full phrase is 33 characters); on its own 'generadores' could be momentarily unclear.
- short_pitch and desktop_short_pitch compress 'You set the priorities; your colony does the work' to 'Tú decides; tu colonia trabaja' to stay within 170/270 (168 and 268 used) instead of cutting the sentence outright; faithful in sense but not literal.
- play_short_description drops 'offline' (first item in the cut order) to keep the full colony/unit contrast in 74/80 characters; appstream_summary drops 'rivals' ('lucha por el territorio') to fit 78; ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero drop 'colony' ('Estrategia en tiempo real') because the genre term plus 'colonia' exceeds the limits.
- ios_keywords (100/100 bytes) now carries three offline search forms (sin internet, sin conexión, offline) plus LAN, and leaves out map editor, economy, tactics and skirmish; the choice reflects my judgement of Spanish search habits, not measured search-volume data.
- mac_tail writes 'Mac con chip de Apple (Apple silicon)' with no article, to avoid the un Mac / una Mac regional split; Apple's own Spanish uses 'chip de Apple'.
- multiplayer_heading 'Juega en compañía.' is correct but slightly formal; a native marketer might prefer 'Juega con otros.'
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left exactly as the reviewed storefront-v1 text, as the field map requires.
- The composed-description limits for es could not be confirmed by the checker itself, because it skips that stage while other locales still have errors; I computed them separately with the tool's own composition code (Google Play 3403/4000, App Store 3315/4000, Mac 3259/4000, Microsoft 3525/10000, Amazon 2859/4000, Epic 3034/10000).

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the classes named in the task were found (maps, colony, globs, loot boxes, premium currency, in-app purchases, landscape vs orientation, ranked/casual, rooms, computer opponent vs AI agent are all rendered correctly and consistently; building names match the game's own texts.es.txt: posada, colmena, escuela, circuito de entrenamiento, piscina, cuartel, hospital, torre de defensa, muralla, bandera de guerra, paisaje, Configuración). No price word in the restricted fields, no platform named in Apple-facing fields, all protected terms and numbers intact, all limits respected by actual count.

### eu — Basque

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [eu.md](../translations/eu.md). Review record: [reviews/eu.json](../translations/reviews/eu.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (3): `feature_scripting`, `economy_body`, `desktop_short_pitch`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (3)**

- feature_scripting: 'Idatzi zeure ordenagailuko aurkaria' parsed as 'write the opponent of your own computer' (the possessive attached to 'computer', not to the opponent); rewritten as 'Idatzi zeuk ordenagailuko aurkari bat JavaScript lengoaian'
- economy_body: 'zirkuitoek' is a misspelling of the Basque word for racetrack (standard 'zirkuitu'); corrected to 'zirkuituek'
- desktop_short_pitch: 'estrategia-joko doakoa' put the price word after the noun, which is non-standard word order; changed to 'doako estrategia-jokoa' (length unchanged, 265 of 270)

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- When I opened eu.md it already held a full 78-field storefront-v2 translation with the v2 preamble, not the v1 text the task described; I reviewed that text and left the preamble as it was because it already states v2, 2026-11-24 and AI translation with AI review.
- 'AI agentea' keeps the English abbreviation AI. Basque media and Google's Basque interface often use 'AA' (adimen artifiziala). I kept AI because the game's own Basque catalog uses it and MCP users know it; a native speaker should confirm.
- Racetrack: the game's building label is 'Zirkuitoa' (non-standard spelling) while the game's own tutorial text uses 'zirkuituak'. The listing now uses the standard spelling, so it differs by one letter from the in-game label; the game catalog itself should probably be fixed.
- 'Ordenagailuko aurkari' (computer opponent) is used consistently and stays clearly distinct from 'AI agentea', but a native speaker should confirm it reads as a computer-controlled opponent and not as an opponent playing on a computer; 'ordenagailuak kontrolatutako aurkari' is the unambiguous longer form and does not fit the 28-character overlay.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal ('Iragarki eta erosketa gabe') and trailer_line_4 ('Iragarkirik ez. Erosketarik ez.') say 'purchases' without 'in-app': the store term 'aplikazioko erosketak' does not fit in 28 or 32 characters. The long fields use the full term.
- play_short_description drops 'offline', following the field map's cut order; ios_subtitle has no room for 'colony' (28 of 30 characters).
- 'Loot boxik gabe' attaches the Basque partitive directly to the loanword, which follows the norm but may look odd; 'Ordainpeko monetarik gabe' (no paid currency) should be checked for how players actually say premium currency.
- 'lagunartekoak' for casual matches follows the game's own interface ('Lagunartekoa') but sits next to 'gonbidatu lagunak' (invite friends) in multiplayer_online_body, which could briefly confuse.
- 'sarbide goiztiarrean' (early access) in itch_downloads_note and 'gameplay trailerra' in trailer_title are plausible gaming usage that I could not verify against a Basque store.
- ios_keywords and windows_search_term_\* are Basque terms (96 of 100 bytes); real Basque-speaking users may search in Spanish, French or English, so their search value is uncertain.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay and trailer_line_ai_coplay ('Zure AI agentearekin batera') have no verb. Adding 'Jokatu' exceeds 28 characters, and dropping 'batera' risks an 'against' reading.
- shot_overlay_economy (28 of 28), trailer_line_3 (32 of 32), shot_caption_fair_deal and shot_caption_ai_coplay (100 of 100) sit exactly at their limits; any later edit must recount.

### fa — Persian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [fa.md](../translations/fa.md). Review record: [reviews/fa.json](../translations/reviews/fa.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (26): `fair_deal_line`, `play_short_description`, `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_heading`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `shot_overlay_fair_deal`, `shot_overlay_ai_coplay`, `shot_caption_hero`, `shot_caption_build`, `shot_caption_opponents`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`, `trailer_line_4`, `trailer_line_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (7)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal: 'بی تبلیغ و خرید درون‌برنامه' can be read as 'ad-free, and in-app purchases' (the 'بی' binds only to the first noun), i.e. the opposite of the claim. Rewritten as 'نه تبلیغ نه خرید درون‌برنامه' (28/28), which cannot be misread.
- trailer_line_4: same scope ambiguity ('بی تبلیغ و خرید درون‌برنامه‌ای.'). Rewritten as 'نه تبلیغ نه خرید درون‌برنامه‌ای.' (32/32).
- fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal: 'premium currency' was 'ارز پولی' (literally 'money currency'), unclear outside hardcore gaming circles and open to a foreign-exchange reading. Changed to 'ارز درون‌بازی پولی' (paid in-game currency) in both fields; the caption was restructured under a single 'بدون' to stay within 100 (now 94).
- multiplayer_online_body: 'اتاق بازی' reads as a children's playroom; changed to 'میزبان یک اتاق شوید'. Also 'به‌صورت مهمان آنلاین' (parsable as 'as an online guest') changed to the game's own 'به‌عنوان مهمان'.
- defense_body: passive 'جنگ ... برده می‌شود' can be read as 'the war is carried/taken' rather than 'won'; rewritten in the active voice ('جنگ را همان‌قدر در مسافرخانه‌ها می‌برید که در خط مقدم').
- shot_caption_hero: 'کلونی کامل' meant 'a complete colony', not 'a whole colony'; changed to 'کل کلونی'.
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'همان کلونی' ('that same colony') had no antecedent in a stand-alone bullet; now 'یک کلونی مشترک' in the bullet and 'همان کلونی خودتان' in the body.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- Colony term: the listing uses 'کلونی' (as the reviewed v1 listing did; it suits a creature colony and avoids the colonialism connotation), but the game's own Persian UI uses 'مستعمره' throughout (165 occurrences, 0 of 'کلونی'). A native speaker should decide whether the listing should match the in-game word.
- 'globs' is rendered 'گلوبول‌ها', matching the game's Persian tutorial text. It sits close to 'گلبول' (blood cell); a native speaker should confirm it reads as a creature name.
- 'premium currency' = 'ارز درون‌بازی پولی': clear, but a native games marketer may prefer another community form (for example 'ارز پولی بازی' or 'ارز خریدنی').
- shot_overlay_fair_deal uses the short form 'خرید درون‌برنامه' (not Google Play's full 'خرید درون‌برنامه‌ای') and no comma, because the full form does not fit in 28 characters. trailer_line_4 uses the full term but also drops the comma to keep its final full stop within 32.
- Genre term spelling: visible text uses the orthographically correct 'هم‌زمان' (with zero-width non-joiner); windows_search_term_1 deliberately uses the more commonly typed 'همزمان'. Whether store search treats the two as the same is unverified.
- ios_subtitle / shot_overlay_hero 'استراتژی هم‌زمان کلونی‌محور' (colony-centred real-time strategy) is accurate but slightly coined; a native check of its appeal is worthwhile.
- ios_keywords (99 bytes) includes 'استراتژیک', the adjective form players search for; it is a different token from the subtitle's 'استراتژی' but the same genre concept. Covers RTS, offline, no ads, war, battle and multiplayer only; base building, LAN, map editor, economy and tactics were left out for space.
- shot_overlay_territory / 'گسترش یابید' is grammatical but a little formal; the heading and body now use the more natural 'کلونی را کجا گسترش دهید'.
- shot_overlay_maps 'نقشه‌ساز تصادفی' (random map-maker) for 'map generators' is understandable but not an established term.
- epic_legal was kept as the reviewed v1 text per the field map; it begins with the Latin name 'Globulation 2', which can flip paragraph direction to left-to-right in containers that auto-detect direction. Other fields start with a Persian word.
- Several fields sit exactly at their limit (play_short_description 80/80, four overlays 28/28, trailer_line_4 32/32); any later edit must be recounted.
- Platform names (Android, iPhone, iPad, Mac, Windows, macOS, Linux) are kept in Latin script; '1v1' is written '۱ به ۱' and all numbers use Persian digits.
- No human or native-speaker validation: this is an AI review of an AI translation.

Apple's Finnish App Store may use 'sovelluksen sisäiset ostot'; I could not confirm it. A native speaker should decide whether short_pitch (the App Store promotional text) should use Apple's form.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 say 'mikromaksuja' (microtransactions) for in-app purchases, because the store term does not fit 28/32 characters. It is the usual Finnish gaming word, but it is not the store's exact term.

- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Reaaliaikainen strategiapeli' with no 'colony': 'siirtokunta' does not fit within 30/28 characters alongside the genre term.
- feature_replays uses 'uusintoja', the usual Finnish gaming word for replays, while the game's own Finnish menus say 'Toistot'. A native speaker should choose between player convention and matching the UI.

'strategia' is included as the Finnish form of the RTS concept. windows_search_term_5 is deliberately unhyphenated ('offline strategiapeli') as typed in search, which is not standard spelling.
- shot_caption_fair_deal lists the four items without 'eikä' before the last one, because the full form is 102 characters against a limit of 100. No store composes this caption today.
- 'Ne' in 'Sinä päätät. Ne rakentavat.' (overlay and trailer line 1) refers to the creatures in the picture; a native speaker may prefer a noun.
- 'lootboxeja' is the loanword players use; Finnish regulators also say 'yllätyslaatikot'.
- 'Pidä siirtokunta ruokittuna' is correct but slightly translated in feel; 'Pidä siirtokunta ruoassa' is a more idiomatic alternative a native speaker might prefer.
- The whole catalog is AI translation with AI review, not native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; the 10 edits are terminology and fluency fixes. All other fields were checked against the English and left as written.

### fil — Filipino

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [fil.md](../translations/fil.md). Review record: [reviews/fil.json](../translations/reviews/fil.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (33): `headline`, `fair_deal_line`, `short_pitch`, `play_short_description`, `ios_subtitle`, `ios_keywords`, `appstream_summary`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `windows_search_term_1`, `windows_search_term_3`, `windows_search_term_5`, `listing_intro`, `control_heading`, `control_body`, `map_heading`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `features_heading`, `feature_control`, `feature_maps`, `feature_colonies`, `feature_editor`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `shot_overlay_hero`, `shot_overlay_fair_deal`, `shot_caption_hero`, `shot_caption_maps`, `shot_caption_opponents`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`, `trailer_title`, `trailer_line_4`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (11)**

- shot_caption_opponents: 'Ilang kolonya ang nasa iisang mapa...' reads as a question ('How many colonies are on one map...?') because sentence-initial 'Ilang' means 'how many'; rewritten as 'May ilang kolonya sa iisang mapa na nag-aagawan sa parehong lupa.'
- play_short_description: 'offline' had been dropped although it fits; restored as 'Offline real-time strategy. Walang ads. Pamunuan ang kolonya, hindi bawat yunit.' (exactly 80 characters, all four priority elements, no price word)
- headline / play_short_description / ios_subtitle / shot_overlay_hero / short_pitch / desktop_short_pitch / appstream_summary / listing_intro: genre term 'Estratehiya sa real time' is a stiff calque that Filipino players neither say nor search for; replaced consistently with the loan term 'real-time strategy' that players actually type
- fair_deal_line and every 'no ads' field: 'Walang ad' changed to 'Walang ads', the form players use and search for; the catalog was inconsistent (keywords said 'walang ads', every other field 'walang ad')
- appstream_summary: 'rivals' had been dropped ('makipag-agawan ng lupa'); restored 'sa karibal' within the 78 limit (77)
- short_pitch / desktop_short_pitch / control_heading: 'kolonya mo ang gumagawa' / 'Mga glob mo ang gumagawa' left 'do the work' incomplete ('are the ones who make/do'); changed to 'nagtatrabaho'
- multiplayer_online_body: 'kalabang computer na lang ang makakalaban mo' was redundant and clumsy; now 'kalabang computer ang makakaharap mo'
- windows_search_term_1/3/5: literal translations nobody types ('estratehiya sa real time', 'pagtatayo ng base', 'offline na estratehiya') replaced with real queries ('real time strategy', 'base building', 'offline na larong estratehiya')
- ios_keywords: replaced low-value literal terms (hukbo, ekonomiya, mapa, editor, base) with terms players type (giyera, army, battle, base building); 99 of 100 bytes, no price word, no word from the name or subtitle
- feature_colonies / shot_caption_hero: 'match' rendered as 'laro', ambiguous with the game itself; now the loanword 'match', consistent with 'quick match'
- map_body / shot_caption_maps: bare 'yaman' (wealth) for 'resources' changed to 'likas na yaman', the term the game's own Filipino text uses

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- Genre term: I chose the English loan 'real-time strategy' over 'estratehiya sa real time' or Google Play's category word 'Diskarte'. A native speaker should confirm this is the preferred register for the listing; it now leads the headline, short description and hero screenshot overlay.
- In-app purchases: the catalog uses the loan 'in-app purchase' throughout. I believe Google Play's Filipino interface uses 'mga in-app na pagbili' (from memory, not verified). The store term does not fit the 28-character overlay ('Walang ads o in-app na pagbili' is 29), so the loan was kept everywhere for consistency. A native speaker should decide whether the description body should use the store term instead.
- 'Walang ads' (English plural, as players say it) is used instead of the formal-localisation style 'walang ad'; 'in-app purchase' and 'loot box' stay singular. A native editor may prefer one uniform style.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are now just 'Real-time strategy': no idiomatic way to add 'colony' was found within 30/28 characters, so 'colony' was dropped as the field map allows. A native marketer may find a natural phrasing that includes 'kolonya'.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and uses three short sentences rather than the English two, to keep 'offline' without weakening the colony-not-units contrast.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal (28/28), shot_overlay_build (28/28) and shot_caption_economy (100/100) sit exactly at their limits; any future edit must recount.
- windows_search_term_1 was a 'renamed' field whose reviewed v1 translation ('estratehiya sa real time') I replaced with 'real time strategy'; the Filipino word 'estratehiya' is kept in term 5. Terms 1, 2, 3 and 6 are English because that is how Filipino-speaking players search; someone with Microsoft Store search data for the Philippines should confirm.
- The 67 'landscapes' are 'tanawin' and handmade maps are 'mapang gawa ng kamay', following the game's own Filipino text (keys [Landscape] and [handmade map]); both sound slightly literal in a store listing but match what players see in the menus.
- 'premium currency' is kept as the English loan in fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal; gamers know it, but a casual reader may not. The glossary's fallback ('no currency to buy') was not used.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay / trailer_line_ai_coplay 'Kakampi mo ang AI agent mo' ('Your AI agent is your teammate') is a statement rather than the English imperative; the literal 'Maglaro kasama ang AI agent mo' is 31 characters and does not fit the overlay.
- shot_overlay_economy ('Pakainin ang kolonya') differs from trailer_line_2 and economy_heading ('Panatilihing busog ang kolonya'), which is 30 characters and does not fit the 28-character overlay.
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left as the reviewed storefront-v1 text, as the field map requires; I read them and found no errors but did not rework them.
- This is an AI review, not native-speaker validation. No Filipino speaker has read the catalog.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- Typography: I replaced the plain space before every colon and semicolon with a no-break space (U+00A0), 18 occurrences, in all fields except the unchanged epic_tail and epic_legal. If a store console normalises U+00A0 to a plain space, saved-value readback will show a whitespace-only difference. The preamble now notes this.
- Gender: the game's French catalog makes workers and warriors feminine (ouvrières, guerrières) while 'globs' is masculine, so shot_overlay_build and trailer_line_1 say 'Ils bâtissent' and the captions say 'elles'. A native speaker should confirm the generic 'Ils' on the standalone overlay.

- ios_keywords (98 bytes) uses 'wifi' (for the 'sans wifi' offline search) and 'gestion' (the French search equivalent of economy games). LAN, map editor and skirmish are left out. These are judgment calls, not checked against App Store search data.
- play_short_description drops 'hors ligne' per the cut order; no version with it fits in 80 characters while keeping 'sans publicité' and the colony/unit contrast.
- shot_overlay_opponents '8 adversaires ordinateur' is telegraphic noun apposition forced by the 28-character limit; a native check is worthwhile.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 cartes, 67 générateurs' relies on 'cartes' for the reader to understand what the generators produce.
- multiplayer_online_body keeps 'amicale' for casual and 'partie rapide' for quick match because the game's French interface uses exactly those words (Classée / Amicale / Partie rapide / Salon / Invité). 'Amicale' could be read as 'with friends'.
- Three fields sit exactly at their limit (appstream_summary 78/78, shot_overlay_battle 28/28, shot_caption_economy 100/100) and short_pitch is 168/170, desktop_short_pitch 268/270: any later edit must be recounted.
- feature_tutorial uses 'didacticiel' to match the game's catalog, although many players say 'tutoriel'.
- This is an AI review, not native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found: the catalog at translations/fr.md already held a complete storefront-v2 translation (78 fields), not the v1 text the task described. My edits were fluency and typography fixes only.

**Serious errors found and fixed (9)**

- multiplayer_online_body: 'room' was rendered as 'कमरा' (a physical room in a house), not an online lobby; changed to the gamer term 'रूम'
- multiplayer_online_body: 'ranked or casual' was paraphrased as 'with rank / without rank' and 'quick match' as the stilted 'त्वरित मैच'; replaced with the standard competitive-gaming terms 'रैंक्ड', 'कैज़ुअल', 'क्विक मैच'
- multiplayer_online_body: 'कंप्यूटर प्रतिद्वंद्वी से खेलते हैं' was ambiguous between playing with and against; now 'के ख़िलाफ़ खेलते हैं'
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'play the same colony' was the unidiomatic calque 'कॉलोनी खेलें / खेल सकता है'; changed to 'कॉलोनी सँभालें / सँभाल सकता है'
- multiplayer_crossplay / multiplayer_crossplay_apple: 'उन्हीं ऑनलाइन मैचों में' ('those same matches') had no antecedent in a field that stands alone; reworded to 'एक ही ऑनलाइन मैच में'
- economy_body: number agreement error, singular noun 'सराय' with plural verb 'देती हैं'; now 'सरायें'
- feature_control: 'अपने आप काम करते हैं' read as 'work automatically' rather than 'do that work themselves'; now 'वह काम ख़ुद कर लेते हैं'
- map_body / feature_maps: 'generate a map' was 'नक्शा बनाएँ', identical to the game's map-editor 'create map' string; now 'जनरेट करें / जनरेट होने वाले', matching the overlay's 'नक्शा जनरेटर'
- terminology (7 fields): workers were 'कामगार' while the game's own Hindi UI calls the unit 'श्रमिक'; aligned to the in-game name (same length, limits unaffected)

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- 'Colony' is 'कॉलोनी' throughout (as in the reviewed v1 listing, and the searchable genre word), but the game's Hindi UI says 'बस्ती' (112 occurrences). In everyday Hindi 'कॉलोनी' also means a residential neighbourhood. A native speaker should decide whether to keep the loanword or switch to 'बस्ती'; a switch would need the genre tag in ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero rewritten.
- 'Unit' is 'इकाई' (matches the game UI); many Hindi gamers say 'यूनिट'. Worth a native check for headline and play_short_description.
- 'प्रीमियम करेंसी' and 'लूट बॉक्स' are loanwords in fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal; a native speaker should confirm 'प्रीमियम करेंसी' is understood as paid currency by casual players (the glossary's fallback is 'no currency to buy').
- Genre spelling 'रियल-टाइम' (common media spelling) was kept; Google and Microsoft localisations often write 'रीयल-टाइम'. Search matching may differ.
- ios_keywords (99 of 100 bytes: RTS, युद्ध, सेना, जंग, ऑफलाइन, बिना विज्ञापन) and the windows_search_term_\* values are an AI estimate of what Hindi speakers type; no search-volume data was checked. Many Hindi users search in Latin script. 'बिना विज्ञापन' is kept as a two-word phrase with an internal space.
- Hidden search fields (ios_keywords, windows_search_term_5) deliberately use the commonly typed 'ऑफलाइन' without nukta, while visible copy uses 'ऑफ़लाइन'.
- trailer_line_3 uses 'विरोधियों' instead of the catalog's usual 'प्रतिद्वंद्वियों' because the latter makes the line 39 characters against a limit of 32; it is exactly 32 now.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay and trailer_line_ai_coplay drop 'your' ('AI एजेंट के साथ मिलकर खेलें') to fit 28 characters while keeping 'मिलकर' (same side); 'your own' is kept in ai_heading, ai_body, feature_ai_coplay and desktop_short_pitch.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal is exactly 28 code points and ios_subtitle is now 27 of 30 (added 'गेम'); Devanagari conjuncts can render wider than the count suggests, so the rendered screenshots should be checked visually.
- Tutorial is 'ट्यूटोरियल' (store convention) while the game menu says 'प्रशिक्षण', which the listing uses for 'training'; single-player is 'सिंगल-प्लेयर' while the game says 'एकल-खिलाड़ी'; the listing says 'गेम' where the game UI says 'खेल'.
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left byte-identical to the reviewed v1 text, as the field map requires; they were not re-edited.
- The task described hi.md as still holding v1 text, but it already held a full storefront-v2 draft with a compliant preamble when the review began; the preamble needed no change.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the 'maps as playing cards' class, no added claims, no price word in a restricted field and no platform named in Apple-facing fields were found in the translator's draft; the fixes above are terminology, idiom and grammar.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (9)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 say 'Nincs reklám, nincs vásárlás' (bare 'no purchases'). The standard store term 'alkalmazáson belüli vásárlás' is itself 28 characters, so the 'in-app' qualifier cannot fit beside 'no ads' in 28 characters; 'Se reklám, se mikrotranzakció.' fits the 32-character trailer line but not the overlay (29) and is not a store term. A native speaker or the claims owner should confirm the unqualified wording is acceptable, since the glossary warns against widening to 'nothing to buy'.

- 'kolóniastratégia' in ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero is a coined but natural compound; because the App Store will not index 'stratégia' out of it, 'stratégia' and 'stratégiai' are carried in ios_keywords. A native ASO check of the keyword list (currently exactly 100 bytes; 'multiplayer', map editor and economy left out) would be worthwhile.
- 'föld' is used consistently for 'land' ('Harcolj a földért'); 'terület' is the more common strategy-game word but does not fit trailer_line_3 (already exactly 32 characters). Native check of which reads better on a title card.
- Several fields sit exactly at their limit (shot_overlay_hero, _economy, _territory, _fair_deal, _ai_coplay at 28; trailer_line_3 at 32; short_pitch at 169 of 170; ios_keywords at 100 bytes), so any later wording change must be recounted.
- 'AI-ügynök' was kept rather than 'MI-ügynök'; both are current in Hungarian, and the Latin 'AI' matches the search term and the MCP audience.
- feature_tutorial uses the standard gaming term 'oktatómód', while the game's own Hungarian menu labels the tutorial 'Gyakorlás'.
- The catalog on disk was already a storefront-v2 translation (78 fields), not the v1 text the task described; I reviewed it as found. This is AI review, not native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the maps/playing-cards class, no forbidden price word, no other platform in Apple-facing fields, no broken limit and no protected-term damage were found; the remaining edits were naturalness fixes (bureaucratic 'rendelkezik' and 'kijelölendő', 'terv' for project, negated list with 'vagy', mixed meccs/mérkőzés in one sentence, 'érintéses' to 'érintőképernyős', 'teljes' to 'teljes értékű').

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [it.md](../translations/it.md). Review record: [reviews/it.json](../translations/reviews/it.json).

- shot_overlay_fair_deal: 'Senza pubblicità né acquisti' dropped 'in-app', widening 'no in-app purchases' to 'no purchases', which the glossary and field map forbid; replaced with 'No annunci o acquisti in-app' (28 characters, keeps the store term 'acquisti in-app'; 'annunci' is Google Play's own Italian word for ads)
- trailer_line_4: same widening ('Senza pubblicità né acquisti.'); replaced with 'No pubblicità o acquisti in-app.' (32 characters)

- shot_overlay_fair_deal / trailer_line_4: the 28- and 32-character limits force the telegraphic 'No ...' form, and the overlay says 'annunci' where every other field says 'pubblicità'. A native marketer should confirm 'No annunci o acquisti in-app' reads well on a screenshot; the fluent 'Senza pubblicità né acquisti' fits but drops the 'in-app' scope.
