It is accurate and fits the tight fields; 'territorio' is the more usual strategy-game word but would push short_pitch, appstream_summary and trailer_line_3 over their limits. 'Contendi' as an imperative is correct but slightly literary.
- 'AI agent' is 'agente IA' throughout (the game's own Italian text uses IA). Italian tech readers also write 'agente AI'; for windows_search_term_7 a native should judge which form people actually type.
- 'your own AI agent' is 'il tuo agente IA personale'; a native should confirm 'personale' reads as 'your own, brought by you' and not as a product category.
- ios_keywords: I replaced 'tattica' with 'internet' so that 'senza' pairs into both 'senza internet' and 'senza pubblicità' (now exactly 100 bytes). This rests on my judgement of Italian search habits, not on search-volume data.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Strategia in tempo reale' without 'colony', which does not fit in 30/28 characters; this follows the field map's cut order.
- play_short_description omits 'offline' (78 of 80 characters), following the cut order.
- 'casual' is 'amichevoli', while the game's own menu label for that queue is 'Non classificata'; I kept 'amichevoli' as the more natural listing term.
- No native Italian speaker has validated this catalog; the review is AI only.

### ja — Japanese

Rating: **Publishable**. Catalog: [ja.md](../translations/ja.md). Review record: [reviews/ja.json](../translations/reviews/ja.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (14): `play_short_description`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_heading`, `map_body`, `features_heading`, `feature_maps`, `feature_editor`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `shot_overlay_territory`, `shot_caption_hero`, `shot_caption_opponents`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (5)**

- map_body: the opening clause '小麦、木材、石が欲しい場所にある…' could be parsed as 'a place that wants stone'; rewritten so the three resources are clearly the subject ('小麦、木材、石は、欲しい場所にはなかなかありません').
- listing_intro: '訓練し' read as globs training others rather than being trained; changed to '訓練を積み'.
- shot_caption_opponents: 'マップを分け合い' implied colonies amicably dividing the map, the opposite of competing for it; changed to 'ひとつのマップの上で … 競い合います'.
- shot_caption_hero: '労働者と建物と戦士が集まるコロニー' said workers, buildings and warriors 'gather' at a colony, which is not what the English says; changed to 'からなるコロニー全体'.
- multiplayer_online_body: '楽しめます' added an enjoyment promise that the English ('Play online as a guest') does not make; changed to '遊べます'.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- 'computer opponent' is rendered 'コンピューター対戦相手' throughout. It is clear and distinct from 'AIエージェント', but Japanese players more often say 'CPU' (CPU戦, CPU対戦). A native marketer may prefer 'CPU'; I kept 'コンピューター' because the field map asks the overlay to keep the word 'computer'.
- 'loot boxes' is 'ガチャ' and 'premium currency' is '課金通貨'. These are the usual player terms, but 'ガチャ' can also cover non-paid draws and some listings say '有償通貨' or 'ルートボックス'. Worth a native check.
- 'in-app purchases' is 'アプリ内課金' everywhere. The App Store badge reads 'App内課金' and Google Play's reads 'アプリ内購入'; 'アプリ内課金' is the common cross-store term, but confirm it is acceptable on each store.
- The 67 'landscapes' use the game's own term '地形タイプ' in map_body and feature_maps, while shot_overlay_maps says '67種類のランダム生成地形' so the caption stands alone. A native reader should confirm the two read as the same thing.
- 'expand' is now 'コロニーをどこへ広げるか' in map_heading, map_body and shot_overlay_territory. The game's own Japanese text uses '拡張' for expansion; a native reader may prefer that.
- ios_keywords (97 of 100 bytes) and windows_search_term_\* are judgement calls on what Japanese players type, not checked against search-volume data. '戦略' is included as the kanji form of the genre term (the subtitle uses 'ストラテジー'); 'map editor', 'economy', 'army' and 'skirmish' were left out for space. '拠点建設' for 'base building' could arguably be '街づくり' or '基地建設'.
- desktop_short_pitch now says 'オプション機能として…' where feature_ai_coplay keeps the label form 'オプション：'. Both carry 'optional', but the wording differs between prose and bullet.
- open_source_body renders 'over two decades' as '20年にわたって', which introduces the digit 20 that the English spells out. It is accurate, but a native reader should confirm it does not read as '20年以上' or imply unbroken development.
- '一体ずつ' (one by one) is written in kanji in headline, play_short_description, control_body and feature_control to avoid adding a digit; some readers may find '1体ずつ' easier to read.
- features_heading is now 'ゲームの特徴。' (was 'ゲームの内容。'), a standard listing heading but slightly less literal than 'What's in the game.'
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left byte-identical to the committed storefront-v1 text, as the field map requires, and were not re-edited.
- This is AI translation with independent AI review, not human or native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the classes named in the brief were found (maps, free, computer opponent vs AI agent, premium currency, landscape, rooms, ranked/casual); the translator's draft was already sound and most of my 14 edits are naturalness polish, so a native reader should confirm they are improvements.

### ko — Korean

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ko.md](../translations/ko.md). Review record: [reviews/ko.json](../translations/reviews/ko.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (18): `ios_keywords`, `appstream_summary`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `features_heading`, `feature_control`, `feature_replays`, `feature_offline`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `shot_caption_maps`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (3)**

- shot_caption_maps: '지형, 자원, 길이 다른' was ambiguous in writing: '길이' reads as 'length' as easily as 'routes (길) + subject particle', so the caption could mean 'maps of different terrain, resources and length'. Reworded to '지형, 자원, 경로가 저마다 다른'.
- shot_caption_fair_deal: was two sentences where the field map requires one plain caption sentence; merged into one without changing the four facts.
- feature_control: an imperative followed by a colon ('지휘하세요: ...') is not Korean bullet style, and '스스로 일을 해냅니다' leaned towards a promise of success ('pull it off'); rewritten as a nominal bullet lead with '일은 일꾼과 전사가 스스로 합니다'.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (10)**

- '식민지' for colony follows the game's own Korean UI and the v1 catalog, but the word carries historical weight in Korean and some colony-sim listings prefer '콜로니' or '군락'; a native marketer should confirm it is the right choice for store copy and for windows_search_term_4.
- In-app purchases is rendered '인앱 구매' (Google Play's term and common player usage). Apple's own Korean term is '앱 내 구입'; the catalog has one shared field set, so Apple-facing text (short_pitch as promotional text, fair_deal_line) uses the Google/player term. Understandable, but not Apple's exact wording.
- '글롭' is used for globs as a direct transliteration of the listing's word, while the game's Korean UI calls the creatures '글로뷸' (its English UI says 'globules'). Consistent within the listing, but a native reviewer or the game translators should decide whether the listing should match the in-game '글로뷸'.
- Maps are '지도' and the editor '지도 편집기' to match the game UI; Korean gamers more often say '맵' and '맵 에디터'. Kept for consistency with what players see in the game.
- '확률형 아이템' (loot boxes) and '유료 재화' (premium currency) are the regulatory/player terms; '확률형 아이템' is slightly broader than loot boxes (covers gacha generally). Believed to be the right reading, worth a native check.
- open_source_body renders 'over two decades' as '20년에 걸쳐', which necessarily writes a digit number (20) that is not a digit in the English; Korean has no natural 'decade' word. The checker accepts it.
- ios_keywords and windows_search_term_\* are chosen from judgement about Korean search habits, not from search-volume data; 'LAN' and '경제' are probably low-volume in Korean. An ASO check with real data would help.
- '내 AI 에이전트' uses the Korean UI convention of '내' for 'your own'; '빠른 대전' (quick match) is the game's own term although it literally contains 'fast'.
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left as the reviewed storefront-v1 text per the field map; '개인정보 보호정책' is acceptable, though '개인정보 처리방침' is the more usual Korean legal term.
- The review-record command also wrote translations/reviews/ko.json (the tool's own output), in addition to my edits to ko.md.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the maps/playing-cards, tabletop, free-as-freedom or computer-opponent/AI-agent class were found: the incoming v2 draft was accurate, kept '컴퓨터 상대' and 'AI 에이전트' distinct, kept landscape ('지형 유형') apart from orientation ('세로 또는 가로 화면'), had no price word in restricted fields, no platform names in Apple-facing fields, and all limits already held. The other edits are naturalness and consistency improvements (싱글플레이 spelling aligned with the game UI and with 멀티플레이; '훈련시키고'; '성장하고'; '게임 특징.' as the standard features heading; '리플레이 저장 및 시청'; clearer 'works offline' and 'no accounts' wording; 'what is worth fighting for' no longer implies capture only; ai_body 'It is optional' and 'complete without one' made less abrupt; open_source_body '2001년에 개발을 시작해'; appstream_summary turned into a natural noun phrase; ios_keywords '기지건설' split into '기지,건설' for broader matching, 98 bytes).

### mr — Marathi

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [mr.md](../translations/mr.md). Review record: [reviews/mr.json](../translations/reviews/mr.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (14): `headline`, `short_pitch`, `windows_search_term_3`, `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `feature_colonies`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_lan_body`, `ai_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `shot_caption_maps`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- Genre term: 'रिअल-टाइम रणनीती' is used everywhere. Many Marathi players search in English or with the loanword 'स्ट्रॅटेजी'; a native marketer should confirm 'रणनीती' is the better store search term.
- ios_keywords (99 of 100 bytes): 'RTS,ऑफलाइन,जाहिरातमुक्त,युद्ध,लढाई,तळ,LAN'. Only about 7 terms fit in Devanagari. 'तळ' (base) alone is a weak search term, and 'जाहिरातमुक्त' (ad-free) costs 36 bytes; a native speaker may prefer 'सैन्य' (army) or another form of 'no ads'. I am also not sure the App Store has a Marathi localization at all.
- windows_search_term_3: I changed 'तळ बांधणी' to the loanword 'बेस बिल्डिंग' on the judgement that this is what Marathi-speaking players type. That is a judgement call; iOS keywords still use 'तळ'.
- 'play the same colony alongside your AI agent' (feature_ai_coplay, ai_body) is now 'एकाच वसाहतीकडून खेळा' (play on the same colony's side). It avoids the calque 'तीच वसाहत खेळा' and cannot be read as playing against the agent, but states shared control slightly less directly; the rest of ai_body makes it explicit.
- Several terms follow the game's own Marathi strings and may feel formal or unusual to players: 'यादृच्छिक भूदृश्य' (random landscapes), 'हस्तनिर्मित नकाशे' (handmade maps), 'शिकवणी' (tutorial), 'धावपट्टी' (racetrack, normally 'runway'), 'पोळे' (swarm), 'जलद सामना' (quick match; 'जलद' means fast, though the glossary asks for no speed promise). I kept them so the listing matches the interface.
- Mixed vocabulary by design: 'खेळ' for game in body text but 'गेम' in windows_search_term_7 and 'गेमप्ले ट्रेलर'; 'मुक्त स्रोत' in body text but 'ओपन सोर्स' as the search term.
- epic_tail (kept from v1 as the field map requires) says 'initially a guest account is created' rather than 'creates an account, initially a guest account'. Same meaning, slightly compressed; epic_legal has the fuller form.
- 'विनामूल्य' is used for 'free' (unambiguous zero price, matches epic_legal); 'मोफत' would be more colloquial if a native marketer prefers it.
- play_short_description (80/80), shot_overlay_fair_deal (28/28) and trailer_line_3 (32/32) sit exactly at their limits; any later wording change must be recounted.
- shot_overlay_opponents drops the verb ('8 संगणकीय प्रतिस्पर्धी'), as the field map allows, because the verb form is 30 characters.
- This is AI translation with AI review; no native Marathi speaker has validated it.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning, policy or limit errors were found; all 14 edits were fluency and clarity fixes. The catalog was already a storefront-v2 translation when I opened it (not v1 as the task said), with the preamble already carrying the v2 provenance; I only added a note that it was independently reviewed and edited on 2026-11-24.

### ms — Malay

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ms.md](../translations/ms.md). Review record: [reviews/ms.json](../translations/reviews/ms.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (20): `ios_keywords`, `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `feature_opponents`, `feature_tutorial`, `feature_offline`, `multiplayer_heading`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `itch_downloads_note`, `shot_overlay_territory`, `shot_overlay_fair_deal`, `shot_caption_economy`, `shot_caption_battle`, `trailer_line_4`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (3)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal: 'Tiada iklan atau belian apl' said 'no ads or app purchases'. 'belian apl' means buying apps, so it lost the in-app meaning and could read as a price claim, which overlays forbid. Now 'Tiada iklan/belian dalam apl' (28 characters).
- trailer_line_4: same 'belian apl' problem ('Tiada iklan. Tiada belian apl.'). Now 'Tiada iklan & belian dalam apl.' (31 characters).
- feature_opponents: '8 lawan komputer, ...' can be read as '8 versus the computer', because the game itself writes '1 lawan 1' for 1v1. Now 'Hadapi 8 lawan komputer, daripada yang mudah hingga yang sukar', matching the overlay.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal uses a slash ('Tiada iklan/belian dalam apl') because the standard 'pembelian dalam apl' cannot fit 28 characters alongside 'no ads'. A native marketer should confirm the slash is acceptable on a screenshot or suggest a better 28-character wording that keeps 'dalam apl'. trailer_line_4 uses '&' for the same reason.
- 'belian dalam apl' in the overlay and trailer line is a shortened form of the store term 'pembelian dalam apl' used in the body fields; check it reads as the same thing.
- 'mata wang premium' (premium currency) and the loanword 'loot box' should be confirmed as the wording Malaysian players actually use; the glossary's fallback would be 'tiada mata wang untuk dibeli'.
- 'Penginapan' for inn and 'Sarang' for swarm follow the game's Malay catalog, but 'penginapan' means lodging, not a food place. defense_body therefore carries a short gloss, 'penginapan yang membekalkan makanan'. If the in-game term changes, the listing should follow.
- ios_keywords and windows_search_term_\* are best guesses at real Malay search behaviour ('offline', 'tanpa iklan', 'perang', 'bina markas/pangkalan'); no search-volume data was available. Whether 'markas' or 'pangkalan' is the commoner word for a base needs a native check.
- 'berpenarafan' (ranked) and 'kasual' follow the game UI; many Malaysian players say 'ranked' in English.
- 'Ciri pilihan:' renders 'Optional:' using the standard software term, but 'pilihan' can also mean 'selected'; a native reader should confirm it reads as optional in desktop_short_pitch and feature_ai_coplay.
- 'berebut wilayah dengan lawan' (short_pitch, desktop_short_pitch, appstream_summary, trailer_line_3) expresses 'fight rivals for land' as contesting territory rather than with an explicit combat verb; the combat verb 'bertempur' is used in defense_heading and shot_overlay_battle.
- trailer_title says 'treler permainan' (game trailer); 'treler gameplay' is common in Malay gaming media if a closer match to 'gameplay trailer' is wanted.
- 'peta buatan tangan' (handmade maps) is literal and understandable but worth a native check.
- shot_caption_ai_coplay sits at 98 of 100 characters and shot_caption_fair_deal at 99 of 100, so any later rewording must stay tight.
- Recording the review rewrote translations/reviews/ms.json, as the review-record command does; that is the only file touched besides translations/ms.md.
- No native human speaker has validated this catalog; it is AI translation with independent AI review.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No other meaning, policy, protected-term or limit errors found. The remaining edits were polish: 'sibuk bekerja, makan, berlatih dan bertempur' in listing_intro; 'menjelajah' and 'menghasilkan' to match the game's own terms; 'daripada' for 'dari'; 'pelayar web' so browser is not confused with 'pelayan' (server); 'Mod pemain tunggal'; 'lesen GPL'; 'Main bersama-sama.'; a more verbal territory overlay; keywords rebalanced.

### nb — Norwegian Bokmål

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [nb.md](../translations/nb.md). Review record: [reviews/nb.json](../translations/reviews/nb.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (13): `ios_keywords`, `windows_search_term_7`, `control_body`, `feature_offline`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `shot_overlay_opponents`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (11)**

- ios_keywords: contained 'kart' (bare 'map', a poor game search term that mainly matches map apps and kart-racing titles) and 'nett' (ambiguous alone: 'uten nett' = offline but 'nettspill' = online); lacked the plain search word 'strategi'. Replaced with RTS,strategi,strategispill,basebygging,offline,uten,reklame,krig,hær,kamp,flerspiller,LAN,taktikk (98 of 100 bytes).
- amazon_tail: 'spill uten nett og over lokalt nettverk' read as near-contradictory (without net / over local network); now 'uten internett og over lokalt nettverk', and 'verken flerspiller på nett eller kontoer'.
- multiplayer_online_body: 'Hvis ingen spiller venter' can be parsed as 'if nobody plays ...' (spiller = player/plays); now 'Hvis ingen andre spillere venter'.
- android_tail / ios_tail: 'berøringsoppsett' was a coined compound for touch layouts; replaced with the standard 'berøringsgrensesnitt'.
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'spill(e) den samme kolonien' was a calque; now 'styr(e) den samme kolonien sammen med ...', consistent with 'styr kolonien' elsewhere. ai_body: 'Du tar med din egen KI-agent' replaced by idiomatic 'Du stiller med din egen KI-agent'.
- open_source_body: 'sluppet offentlig' was unidiomatic; now 'gjort offentlig tilgjengelig i 2004'.
- feature_offline: clumsy 'Enspiller uten nett, og du trenger ingen konto' tightened to 'Enspiller uten nett og uten konto'.
- mac_tail: 'Du trenger en Mac' changed to standard store phrasing 'Krever en Mac med Apple-chip.'
- control_body: repeated 'velger' in two senses (set a number / select units); first one changed to 'bestemmer'.
- shot_overlay_opponents: restored the verb, 'Møt 8 datastyrte motstandere' (exactly 28 characters), so the caption is a complete phrase.
- windows_search_term_7: changed hidden search term from 'MCP KI-agent spill' to 'MCP AI-agent spill' because 'AI' is typed far more often than 'KI' in Norwegian searches; visible copy keeps 'KI-agent' (matches the game's own nb text and Språkrådet usage). Noted in the preamble.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (10)**

- KI vs AI: visible copy uses 'KI-agent' (official Norwegian usage, matches the game's nb strings); many Norwegian tech readers say 'AI-agent'. A native marketer should confirm the choice, and whether the hidden search term 7 should stay 'AI-agent' while the listing says 'KI-agent'.
- 'premiumvaluta' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal): used in Norwegian games press, but a native speaker should confirm it is clear to casual mobile players; the fallback would be 'ingen valuta å kjøpe' (the caption is already at exactly 100 characters, so it would need rewording).
- ios_subtitle / shot_overlay_hero 'Kolonistrategi i sanntid' mirrors the English 'Real-time colony strategy' but does not contain 'sanntidsstrategi' or 'strategi' as standalone search tokens; I compensated with 'strategi' and 'strategispill' in ios_keywords. 'Sanntidsstrategi med kolonier' (29 chars) would index the genre term in the subtitle but does not fit the 28-character overlay; ASO judgement call for a human.
- Three fields sit exactly at their limit (shot_overlay_opponents 28/28, shot_overlay_fair_deal 28/28, trailer_line_4 32/32, shot_caption_fair_deal 100/100): check they render without clipping in the caption/trailer renderer.
- 'Land' is rendered two ways by design: indefinite 'land' ('kjemp mot rivaler om land') and definite 'territoriet' ('Kjemp om territoriet'), because 'landet' reads as 'the country'. A native reader may prefer one noun throughout.
- feature_tutorial uses 'Opplæring med 10 leksjoner' while the in-game menu label for the tutorial is 'Veiledning'; 'Opplæring' reads more clearly as a tutorial in a store list, but it differs from the menu word.
- ios_keywords search-volume choices (e.g. 'basebygging', 'hær', 'taktikk', 'LAN') are informed guesses without Norwegian App Store search data.
- trailer_title keeps the loanword 'gameplay-trailer', which is common in Norwegian but could be 'spilltrailer' if a fully Norwegian title is preferred.
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left byte-identical to the reviewed storefront-v1 text as the field map requires; not re-edited.
- Running review-record wrote translations/reviews/nb.json (the tool's own output); nb.md is the only file I edited by hand.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors, false claims, policy violations or broken limits were found in the translator's draft; it already had the 78 v2 fields in order, protected terms and numbers intact, no price word in restricted fields, no platform named in Apple-facing fields, and 'datastyrte motstandere' (computer opponents) kept distinct from 'KI-agent' (AI agent). The changes below are quality fixes, not serious errors.

- shot_overlay_maps "23 kaarten, 67 generatoren" (26/28): "kaartgeneratoren" does not fit in 28 characters, so the bare "generatoren" relies on the preceding "kaarten" for its meaning; a native speaker should confirm it does not read as power generators, or prefer "23 kaarten, 67 landschappen" (27)
- shot_overlay_fair_deal uses "ads" ("Geen ads of in-app aankopen", 27/28) because "Geen reclame of in-app aankopen" is 31 characters; every other field says "reclame". Common Dutch gamer usage, but a deliberate inconsistency to confirm
- trailer_line_4 "Geen reclame of in-app aankopen." is exactly 32/32 and short_pitch is 169/170: no slack for any later edit
- "premiumvaluta" for premium currency (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal): understood by gamers, but a native speaker should confirm it is clearer than e.g. "geen in-game valuta te koop" (the caption has only 2 characters of room)
- "globs" is kept as the creature name per the glossary, but the game's own Dutch tutorial text calls them "bolletjes"; listing and in-game wording therefore differ
- ios_subtitle "Realtime koloniestrategie" is a correct Dutch compound but App Store search will not match the separate words "strategie" or "kolonie" inside it; "strategie" is therefore carried in ios_keywords
- ios_keywords: I added "wifi" (so "zonder wifi" matches, the form Dutch players use to search for offline games) and kept "strategie"; both are search forms of listed concepts (offline, RTS) rather than literal list items, and the list is exactly 100 bytes. Battle, skirmish and map editor concepts were left out for space
- "snelle 1v1-wedstrijden" follows the game's own Dutch UI term for quick match ("Snel spelen" / "Snelle wedstrijd") but, like the English, contains a speed word the glossary cautions about
- "Herhalingen" (replays) and "Competitief/Vrij" (ranked/casual) follow the game's Dutch catalog; many Dutch players would say "replays" and "ranked"
- "realtime strategie" written as two words (common store/search usage); the strict official compound would be "realtimestrategie"
- "Houd je kolonie gevoed" (heading, overlay, trailer) is correct Dutch but slightly calqued; a native marketer might prefer "Geef je kolonie te eten"

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No policy, protected-term, price-word or limit violations were found in the incoming draft; computer opponent (computertegenstander) and AI agent (AI-agent) were already kept distinct, screen orientation (staand of liggend) and random-map landscapes (landschappen) already used different words
