### pt-BR — Portuguese — Brazil

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [pt-BR.md](../translations/pt-BR.md). Review record: [reviews/pt-BR.json](../translations/reviews/pt-BR.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (8): `ios_keywords`, `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `feature_offline`, `feature_scripting`, `multiplayer_heading`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (4)**

- control_body: 'coletam os materiais, constroem e comem quando têm fome' ran building and eating together, so it could be read as 'they build and eat when hungry'; rewritten so building and eating are separate statements, as in the English.
- feature_scripting: 'adversário controlado pelo computador em JavaScript' attached 'in JavaScript' to 'controlled by the computer'; reordered to 'Programe em JavaScript seu próprio adversário controlado pelo computador'.
- feature_offline: 'Modo para um jogador offline' could be read as 'a mode for one offline player'; reordered to 'Modo offline para um jogador'.
- ios_keywords: the list was close to a literal rendering of the English (tática, economia) and omitted the forms Brazilians actually type for offline and no-ads games; replaced with 'RTS,offline,sem,internet,anúncios,propaganda,guerra,base,construção,exército,batalha,multiplayer' (exactly 100 UTF-8 bytes, no price word, no words from the name or subtitle, concepts from the allowed list only).

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- shot_overlay_opponents '8 adversários de computador' (27 of 28 characters): kept because the field map requires the number 8 and 'computer', and the full form 'adversários controlados pelo computador' does not fit. A native speaker should confirm it reads as computer-controlled opponents and not as 'opponents who play on PC'; 'do computador' was rejected because it reads as 'opponents of the computer'. 'Enfrente 8 bots' would be the clearest alternative if dropping the word 'computador' is acceptable.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 mapas, 67 geradores': 'geradores' stands without 'de mapas' because the full phrase is 30 characters. A native speaker should confirm it is understood as map generators on the image.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and uses the shortened contrast 'Comande a colônia, não unidades' in order to keep 'offline'. The field map's cut order would drop 'offline' first; the alternative 'Estratégia em tempo real sem anúncios. Comande a colônia, não cada unidade.' (74) keeps the full contrast. I kept the translator's version because both ideas survive and 'offline' is a strong search term in Brazil; a human should decide.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal 'Sem anúncio ou compra no app' (28) uses the singular to fit; the plural form with 'nem' is 29 to 32 characters. Natural in colloquial Brazilian Portuguese but worth a native glance. trailer_line_4 uses the plural 'Sem anúncios nem compras no app.' (same facts, different wording).
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Estratégia em tempo real' without 'colônia', because 'Estratégia de colônia em tempo real' is 35 characters (limit 30 and 28).
- 'compras no app' is Google Play's Brazilian term; Apple's Brazilian App Store writes 'compras dentro do app'. One term is used across all stores for consistency and length.
- 'moeda premium' is the usual Brazilian gaming term for paid currency, but a native speaker should confirm that non-gamers do not find it unclear ('sem moeda para comprar' is the fallback the glossary allows).
- ios_keywords: 'sem internet' and 'sem propaganda' were chosen from my knowledge of Brazilian search habits, not from App Store search-volume data; 'tática', 'economia', 'LAN' and the map-editor concept were left out to fit 100 bytes.
- The game's own Brazilian strings call computer opponents 'Adversários de IA' / 'oponente IA'; the listing deliberately uses 'adversários controlados pelo computador' to keep them distinct from 'agente de IA', as the glossary requires. This differs from the in-game wording.
- epic_tail and epic_legal say 'do Globulation 2' (with article) while the new fields use the name without an article at sentence start; both are natural, left unchanged because those two fields keep their reviewed storefront-v1 text.
- The task description said the file still held the storefront-v1 translation; when I opened it, it already held a complete storefront-v2 translation (78 fields, v2 preamble), which is what I reviewed.
- Recording the review also rewrote translations/reviews/pt-BR.json (done by the review-record tool, not by hand). Nothing was committed.
- This is an AI review, not native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- None of the serious class (wrong meaning, false claim, price word in a forbidden field, other platform named in an Apple field, broken limit, altered protected term) was found: the translator's catalog was already structurally clean and within every limit before my edits. The items below are the meaning/clarity defects I fixed.

### pt-PT — Portuguese — Portugal

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [pt-PT.md](../translations/pt-PT.md). Review record: [reviews/pt-PT.json](../translations/reviews/pt-PT.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (11): `appstream_summary`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `feature_opponents`, `feature_offline`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `ai_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `shot_caption_territory`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (7)**

- multiplayer_crossplay: 'Os jogadores de computador' reads as 'computer players' and could be taken for the computer opponents; rewritten as 'Quem joga no computador, no navegador ou em dispositivos móveis partilha...'
- shot_caption_territory: 'onde as colónias rivais se encontram' is most naturally read as 'where the rival colonies are located', not 'where they meet'; changed to 'onde as colónias rivais fazem fronteira'
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'joga a mesma colónia' / 'jogar a mesma colónia contigo' was an unidiomatic calque of 'play the same colony'; now 'joga na mesma colónia ao lado do teu próprio agente de IA' and 'jogar contigo na mesma colónia'
- economy_body: 'As colmeias produzem mais.' left it unclear what swarms produce; now 'produzem mais globs'
- feature_offline: 'Modo para um jogador offline' could be read as 'a mode for an offline player'; now 'Modo offline para um jogador, sem precisar de conta'
- android_tail / ios_tail: 'disposições táteis' was an unnatural literal rendering of 'touch layouts'; now 'uma interface tátil própria para telemóveis e outra para tablets' (and iPhone/iPad)
- ai_body: 'Liga-o' / 'desligado a menos que o ligues' used the hardware on/off verb for a software setting; now 'Ativa-o nas Opções' / 'fica desativado a menos que o atives' (off-by-default statement kept)

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- The catalog on disk was already a storefront-v2 translation (78 fields), not the v1 text the task described; I reviewed that. No structural, limit, protected-term or price-word violations were found in it; all my fixes are meaning/naturalness.
- shot_overlay_opponents '8 adversários do computador' (27/28): kept because the field map asks to keep 8 and 'computer', but 'adversários do computador' can in theory be parsed as 'opponents of the computer'. A native speaker should confirm it or prefer 'Enfrenta 8 adversários CPU' (26).
- shot_overlay_fair_deal 'Sem anúncios nem compras' (24/28): 'compras na app' does not fit in 28, so the overlay says only 'compras'. It is read as in-app purchases next to 'anúncios', but it is slightly wider than the English. trailer_line_4 carries the full 'Sem anúncios nem compras na app.' (32/32).
- In-app purchases term: 'compras na app' (Google Play pt-PT wording) is used everywhere, including Apple-facing fields; the App Store in Portugal labels them 'compras integradas'. A native marketer may want to confirm one term for all stores is acceptable.
- 'ranked' is rendered 'competitivas' (paired with 'casuais'); 'classificativas' or the loanword 'ranked' are alternatives a native competitive player should weigh.
- 'fight rivals' is 'luta com rivais' throughout (needed to fit trailer_line_3 at 32/32 and appstream_summary at 78/78); 'contra' would be more explicit but does not fit the tight fields, so 'com' was kept everywhere for consistency.
- appstream_summary uses 'expande uma colónia' for 'grow a colony' (the pitches use 'Desenvolve uma colónia') because 'desenvolve' exceeds the 78-character limit; it sits exactly at 78.
- play_short_description drops 'offline' per the field map cut order (75/80; adding it gives 84+). ios_subtitle drops 'colony' (does not fit 30 with the genre term), and 'colónia' was not added to ios_keywords because the field map restricts keyword concepts.
- ios_keywords (98/100 bytes) uses 'sem,internet' alongside 'offline' as the form Portuguese players search; omits LAN, map editor, economy and skirmish for space. Real App Store search-volume data was not consulted.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 mapas, 67 geradores' relies on 'mapas' for context ('geradores de mapas' does not fit 28).
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left as the reviewed storefront-v1 text per the field map ('O Globulation 2 é um software gratuito...' could read more naturally without 'um').
- Intransitive 'expandir' ('Planeia para onde expandir', 'Escolhe para onde expandir') is strategy-game jargon; a purist would want 'expandir a colónia', which does not fit the overlay.
- This is AI review, not native-speaker validation. The tool's plain 'check' does not gate on reviews and skips composed-store limits while other locales have errors, so I verified pt-PT's composed store payloads separately (all within limits) and confirmed 'check --require-review' is clean for pt-PT.

### ro — Romanian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ro.md](../translations/ro.md). Review record: [reviews/ro.json](../translations/reviews/ro.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (9): `listing_intro`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_maps`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal / trailer_line_4: 'Fără reclame, fără achiziții' shortens 'achiziții în aplicație' to 'achiziții' because the full store term (22 characters alone) cannot fit in 28/32. In context it reads as in-app purchases, but a native speaker should confirm it does not read as the wider 'nothing is sold'.
- shot_overlay_maps: 'Hărți: 23. Generatoare: 67' is a label-style workaround, because the grammatical '23 de hărți, 67 de generatoare' is 30 characters. It keeps both numbers and the 'generators' idea but reads like a spec line; '23 de hărți, 67 de peisaje' (27) is a more natural alternative that loses the explicit 'generated' sense.
- shot_overlay_opponents: '8 adversari computerizați' is compact and understood, but less idiomatic than the 'adversari controlați de calculator' used in the body fields, which does not fit in 28 characters. A native speaker may prefer another short form.
- fair_deal_line / shot_caption_fair_deal: 'monedă premium' and 'loot boxuri' are the gamer loan terms. A native speaker should confirm 'monedă premium' is clear to casual mobile players, and whether 'loot boxuri' or 'loot box-uri' is the preferred spelling.
- epic_tail / epic_legal were left as the v1 text, as the field map requires. They say 'cont de oaspete' and 'open-source', while the v2 fields and the game UI use 'invitat' and 'open source'. This is a minor synonym/spelling inconsistency, not a meaning error.
- ios_keywords uses 'fără,internet' as the Romanian search form of 'offline' ('jocuri fără internet') alongside 'offline'; 'map editor', 'tactics' and 'skirmish' were left out for space. The keyword choice rests on judgement about Romanian App Store search habits, not on search-volume data.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Strategie în timp real' without 'colony': every variant that adds 'colonie' exceeds 30 characters.
- appstream_summary joins the two verbs with a comma instead of 'și' to stay within 78 characters (77 used).
- trailer_line_3 'Rivalii vor teritoriul. Luptă.' is an adaptation of 'Fight rivals for the land.', because the literal 'Luptă cu rivalii pentru teritoriu.' is 34 characters against a limit of 32.
- 'globi' is used as the Romanian name for globs (masculine animate plural, declined naturally). The game's own Romanian text has no established term, so a native speaker should confirm it reads well.
- The translation and this review are both AI work; no native human speaker has validated the catalog.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy, protected-term or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; my edits were naturalness and terminology fixes only (e.g. 'hărți făcute de mână' -> 'hărți create manual', 'Îți aduci propriul agent AI' -> 'Vii cu propriul tău agent AI', 'împărțit cu' -> 'jucat alături de', 'îi pregătesc' -> 'îi instruiesc' to match the game's own 'instruire').

### ru — Russian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ru.md](../translations/ru.md). Review record: [reviews/ru.json](../translations/reviews/ru.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (11): `control_body`, `map_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `ai_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `shot_overlay_maps`, `shot_caption_economy`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (3)**

- shot_overlay_maps: "23 карты, 67 видов случайных" was an ungrammatical, dangling phrase ("67 kinds of random" with no noun) that a player could not parse on a screenshot; replaced with "23 карты, 67 генераторов" (24 characters, both numbers kept, says the maps are generated)
- multiplayer_crossplay / multiplayer_crossplay_apple: "участвуют в одних и тех же онлайн-играх" could be read as "take part in the same online games (products)"; reworded to "играют в одних и тех же онлайн-матчах, если версия игры совпадает", consistent with "матч" elsewhere
- shot_caption_fair_deal: "Установленная игра:" was a stilted calque of "The game as installed"; now "Игра после установки:"

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- play_short_description (76/80): "offline" is dropped, following the field map's cut order. No natural wording fits the full genre term, "без рекламы", the colony-not-units contrast and "офлайн" in 80 characters (the closest is 81). A native marketer may prefer to trade the contrast for "офлайн", which is a strong Google Play search term in Russian.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are "Стратегия в реальном времени" (28): "colony" does not fit beside the full genre term in 30/28 characters, so it is omitted, as the field map permits.
- In-app purchases: the catalog uses Apple's Russian term "встроенные покупки"; Google Play's own Russian label is "покупки в приложении". The overlay and trailer line use the shorter "покупок в игре" because neither store term fits in 28/32 characters. A native speaker should confirm one term is acceptable on both stores.
- "Без платной валюты" for "no premium currency" is clear (currency you pay for), but "премиум-валюта" or "донатная валюта" are also common among players; worth a native check.
- defense_body: "войну выигрывают в кабаках не меньше, чем на фронте" keeps the game's own building name "Кабак" (inn). In Russian this reads as "taverns" and gives the line a slightly comic tone. economy_body explains that кабаки feed the globs, so it is understandable; kept deliberately.
- shot_overlay_build / trailer_line_1: "Вы решаете. Они строят." relies on the picture for "они"; "Вы решаете. Колония строит." (27 characters) is a fallback if the referent is unclear on a title card.
- shot_overlay_maps: "67 генераторов" omits "карт" (the full phrase is 29 characters, one over the limit); context makes it clear, but it is elliptical.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are kept as their storefront-v1 text, as the field map requires. They use "учетная запись" / "автономный режим" without the letter ё, while the v2 fields use "аккаунт" / "офлайн" with ё. This is a register and spelling inconsistency confined to the Epic legal text.
- ios_keywords (99 of 100 bytes): "RTS,ртс,офлайн,без,рекламы,интернета,война,армия,база,LAN". The term choice (including "интернета" for "без интернета" as the offline search form, and "ртс"/"LAN" over "битва"/"тактика") is a judgment call, not checked against App Store search-volume data.
- "1v1" is kept as in the source per the copy-numbers-exactly rule; "1 на 1" would be the more native form.
- No human or native-speaker validation has been done; this is an AI review.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the maps-as-playing-cards / tabletop / lost-zero-price / opponents-on-a-computer class were found. Protected terms, numbers, URLs, price-word bans, Apple-facing platform bans and the computer opponent (компьютерный противник) vs AI agent (ИИ-агент) distinction were all already correct. The catalog on disk was already a storefront-v2 translation with a correct preamble, not the v1 text the task described.

### sk — Slovak

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [sk.md](../translations/sk.md). Review record: [reviews/sk.json](../translations/reviews/sk.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (13): `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `defense_body`, `feature_control`, `feature_maps`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `mac_tail`, `shot_caption_battle`, `shot_caption_maps`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (5)**

- map_body: the 67 random landscapes were rendered as '67 náhodných krajín', which in the plural reads as '67 random countries'; reworded to '67 typov náhodnej krajiny' (keeps the game's own word 'krajina' from key [Landscape], removes the 'countries' reading)
- feature_maps: same '67 random countries' ambiguity; reworded to 'zo 67 typov náhodnej krajiny'
- mac_tail: 'Mac s Apple čipom' is not how Apple writes the platform name in Slovak; changed to 'Mac s čipom Apple Silicon', the wording on Apple's Slovak support page
- ai_body and shot_caption_ai_coplay: 'local game' was 'miestna hra', which reads as a regional/neighbourhood game rather than a game running on your own computer; changed to 'lokálna hra', consistent with 'po lokálnej sieti'
- open_source_body: 'Je zadarmo' had the volunteers of the previous sentence as its grammatical antecedent; made the subject explicit ('Hra je zadarmo') and replaced 'budovali' with the usual 'vyvíjali'

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (9)**

- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and uses the imperative 'Veľte kolónii'. It is the correct codified imperative of 'veliť' but looks bookish; a native speaker may prefer 'Velíte kolónii, nie jednotkám.' (fits, but drops 'every').
- 'Bez prémiovej meny' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal): the literal gamer term for premium currency. I judged it understandable to Slovak players; a native speaker should confirm it does not need the plainer 'Bez meny za skutočné peniaze'.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 use 'nákupov v hre' because the store term 'nákupov v aplikácii' (used in every other field) exceeds the 28/32 limits. Same fact, slightly different wording across fields.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Stratégia v reálnom čase' with no 'colony': no natural Slovak phrasing with 'kolónia' fits 30/28 characters. The field map allows this priority.
- ios_keywords (98 bytes) omits 'battle' and 'skirmish' for lack of room, which the field map allows. Actual Slovak App Store search behaviour (for example searching without diacritics, or in English) was not measured.
- 'ranked/casual' is 'hodnotené/nezáväzné' zápasy; 'nehodnotené' or the loanword 'rankované' are alternatives a native competitive player might prefer.
- 'globovia' is declined as a masculine animate noun (globovia, globov) consistently; a native speaker should confirm this reads well as the creature name.
- '67 typov náhodnej krajiny' differs slightly from the in-game menu, which uses bare 'krajina/krajiny'; chosen deliberately to avoid the 'countries' reading.
- Whole catalog is AI translation with AI review; no human or native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the 'maps as playing cards' class were found; the translator's draft was accurate. The remaining edits were for naturalness: economy_body (buildings as grammatical subjects of 'train/heal' rewritten as 'V školách ... cvičia a v nemocniciach sa liečia'), control_body ('neoznačujete' as the RTS term for selecting units), 'routes' as 'trasy' instead of 'cesty' (which suggests built roads) in map_body and shot_caption_maps, defense_body word order, shot_caption_battle ('Bojovníci ... bojujú' repetition), multiplayer_online_body ('sa treba prihlásiť').

### sl — Slovenian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [sl.md](../translations/sl.md). Review record: [reviews/sl.json](../translations/reviews/sl.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (7): `control_body`, `economy_body`, `features_heading`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `ai_body`, `windows_search_term_5`, `windows_search_term_7`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (7)**

- economy_body: 'Restavracije hranijo vaše globe' used the accusative plural 'globe', which is identical to the plural of 'globa' (a fine/penalty), so it could be read as 'Inns feed your fines'; rewritten as 'Vaši globi jedo v restavracijah' (nominative, unambiguous)
- features_heading: 'Kaj je v igri.' is the Slovenian idiom for 'What is at stake.'; changed to 'Kaj ponuja igra.'
- ai_body: 'igra ... je popolna tudi brez njega' reads primarily as 'the game is perfect even without it' (an added quality claim); changed to 'celovita' (complete)
- control_body: 'eno po eno' is a non-standard calque; changed to 'posamič'
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'igrati isto kolonijo' was unidiomatic; aligned with the headline verb ('vodite/vodil isto kolonijo')
- windows_search_term_5: 'strategija brez povezave' is not how players type it; changed to 'offline strategija' (matches play_short_description)
- windows_search_term_7: 'igra MCP agent UI' changed to 'igra MCP AI agent' because Slovenian users type 'AI' in search boxes even though edited text uses 'UI' (hidden field only; visible copy keeps 'agent UI')

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- The file already held a storefront-v2 Slovenian translation when I opened it (not v1 as the task text said); I reviewed that text. No meaning errors of the 'maps as playing cards' class were found; building names, 'pokrajina', 'rangirano/sproščeno', 'hitra tekma', 'soba' and 'posnetki iger' match the game's own Slovenian catalog (texts.si.txt).
- shot_overlay_fair_deal 'Brez oglasov in nakupov' (23) drops 'v aplikaciji/v igri' because 'Brez oglasov in nakupov v igri' is 30 characters (limit 28). Both facts are kept, but 'no purchases' is slightly wider than 'no in-app purchases'. An exact alternative that fits in 28 is 'Oglasov in nakupov v igri ni'; a native marketer should choose. trailer_line_4 uses the fuller 'Brez oglasov in nakupov v igri.'
- shot_overlay_maps '23 zemljevidov, 67 pokrajin' uses the game's menu word for landscapes but does not say the 67 are generated; '67 generatorjev' does not fit in 28 characters.
- 'škatle s plenom' for loot boxes: a native speaker should confirm against 'plenilne škatle' (used in some EU/regulatory Slovenian texts) or the loanword 'loot boxi'.
- 'plačljiva valuta' for premium currency is understandable but not an established gamer term; alternative 'premium valuta'.
- 'agent UI' follows Google/Microsoft Slovenian localisation (UI = umetna inteligenca), but 'UI' can be misread as 'user interface' and many players say 'AI agent'; ai_body expands it once as 'agent umetne inteligence (UI)'. The game's own catalog also uses 'UI' for built-in computer opponents, though the listing always calls those 'računalniški nasprotnik'.
- 'Offline strategija' in play_short_description (exactly 80 characters) uses the loanword as the field map permits; purists would prefer 'brez povezave', which does not fit.
- Building names from the game catalog read oddly in marketing copy ('restavracije' for inns, 'porodnišnice' for swarms: 'vojna se dobi v restavracijah'); kept to match what players see in the game.
- ios_subtitle 'Strategija v realnem času' omits 'colony' (does not fit naturally in 30); ios_keywords is exactly 100 bytes and omits skirmish and map editor.
- 'glob' is declined as a Slovenian masculine noun (globi, globov); a native speaker should confirm this reads well given the homonym 'globa' (fine).
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left as the reviewed storefront-v1 text (verified identical to git HEAD).
- Vikanje (2nd person plural) is used throughout, as in the game's own Slovenian text; not validated by a human native speaker.

### sr-Cyrl — Serbian — Cyrillic

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [sr-Cyrl.md](../translations/sr-Cyrl.md). Review record: [reviews/sr-Cyrl.json](../translations/reviews/sr-Cyrl.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (11): `ios_keywords`, `appstream_summary`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `defense_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `open_source_body`, `itch_downloads_note`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (9)**

- multiplayer_crossplay / multiplayer_crossplay_apple: 'играју заједно у истим онлајн играма' could be read as 'play the same online game titles', and the Apple variant read as 'players on other devices play with each other' with no link to the reader; now 'играју у истим онлајн мечевима' (Apple: '... као и ви').
- multiplayer_online_body: 'casual' was the literal 'опуштене' (relaxed); changed to 'необавезне' (no rating at stake). 'host a room' was 'направите собу', now 'отворите собу'. The sign-in clause had awkward clitic order ('за рангиране мечеве се пријавите').
- defense_body: 'док трају храна и болнице које га подржавају' said the hospitals 'last'; now 'док га подржавају храна и болнице'.
- map_body, feature_maps: 'ручно израђене мапе' did not match the game's own Serbian string for handmade map ('Ручно направљена мапа'); aligned.
- ios_keywords: near-literal list. Replaced 'битка' and 'LAN' with 'интернета', so 'без' + 'интернета' covers 'без интернета', the phrase Serbian players actually use for offline games; 99 of 100 bytes.
- open_source_body: 'Волонтери су је градили' (constructed, as a building) changed to 'стварали'; 'GPL, верзија 3' changed to the grammatical 'GPL верзије 3'.
- itch_downloads_note: clumsy relative clause ('издања ... која се преузимају') rewritten; 'Игра у прегледачу' changed to 'Верзија за прегледач'.
- economy_body: 'Све то тражи исте раднике' smoothed to 'Свему томе требају исти радници'.
- appstream_summary: 'раст колоније' changed to 'развој колоније' to match 'Развијајте колонију' elsewhere; exactly 78 of 78.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (14)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal uses 'огласа' (Google Play's Serbian term) while every other field uses 'реклама', because 'Без реклама и куповина у игри' is 29 characters against a limit of 28. A native speaker should confirm the mixed terms are acceptable or supply a 28-character alternative.
- Overlay and trailer line 4 say 'куповина у игри' (in-game purchases) for length; the long fields use the store term 'куповина у апликацији'. Same fact, two wordings.
- 'глобови' as the transliteration of globs: 'глоба' means a fine (penalty) in Serbian. The inflected forms used are unambiguous, but a native speaker should judge the connotation.
- 'стражаре' (the game's own name for defense towers, 'Стражара') is identical in the accusative plural to 'guards' (people). Kept for consistency with the game UI; context ('градите') disambiguates.
- 'необавезне' for casual and 'рангиране' for ranked: Serbian players mostly use the English loanwords, so a native gamer should confirm the choice (alternatives: 'лежерне', 'нерангиране').
- 'лут-боксова' (hyphenated) and 'премијум валуте' are gamer loanwords; the hyphenation and whether 'премијум валута' is clear to non-gamers deserve a native check.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 of 80 and shortens the contrast to 'Водите колонију, а не јединице' (loses 'every', and uses 'водите' rather than the headline's 'командујете'). A full contrast does not fit even with 'офлајн' dropped, so I kept all four ideas.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero are 'Стратегија у реалном времену' (28) and omit 'colony', which does not fit in 30 and 28.
- trailer_line_3 is nominal ('Борба с ривалима за територију.') because the imperative is 35 characters against a limit of 32.
- epic_tail and epic_legal were left as the reviewed storefront-v1 text per the field map. They are internally inconsistent ('онлајн сервис' vs 'онлајн услуге'; 'офлајн/онлајн' vs 'ван мреже/на мрежи').
- 'рачунарски противник' (computer opponent) is correct and clearly distinct from 'AI агент'; some players would say 'компјутерски противник' or 'бот'. The game UI abbreviates its AI as 'ВИ', which the listing does not use.
- ios_keywords: 'интернета' expresses the offline concept as 'без интернета', which is not literally on the field map's concept list; 'LAN' and 'битка' were dropped to make room. The platform matrix maps sr-Cyrl only to Google Play, Microsoft and the F-Droid tree, so this field and ios_subtitle are not exported to any store today.
- Many Serbian players search in Latin script or English; Cyrillic search terms (windows_search_term_\*) may match fewer real queries. That is a product decision, not a translation error.
- 'гејмплеј трејлер' in trailer_title is colloquial gamer usage; a more formal alternative would be 'трејлер игре'.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning-reversing, policy or limit errors found: the catalog already held a storefront-v2 translation (not v1 as the task stated) and it passed the checker before my edits. The items below are the most material fixes, all moderate.

### sv — Swedish

Rating: **Publishable**. Catalog: [sv.md](../translations/sv.md). Review record: [reviews/sv.json](../translations/reviews/sv.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (8): `ios_keywords`, `control_body`, `shot_caption_build`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `ai_guide`, `open_source_body`, `itch_downloads_note`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- ios_keywords: I replaced 'strid' and 'karta' with 'krigsspel' (now 98 of 100 bytes), on the judgement that Swedish players search compound forms such as 'krigsspel' and 'strategispel'. This is not backed by App Store search-volume data; the battle and map-editor concepts are no longer covered.
- ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero: 'Realtidsstrategi om kolonier' (28 of 28-30) is understandable but slightly flat. 'Kolonistrategi i realtid' reads more elegantly but loses the single search word 'Realtidsstrategi' that the glossary wants shared with the headline. A native marketer should choose.
- 'premiumvaluta' (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal): used in Swedish games writing and kept, but a native reader should confirm casual mobile players understand it; the glossary fallback would be 'ingen valuta att köpa'.
- 'köp i appen' follows Google Play's and Microsoft Store's Swedish term; the Swedish App Store label is 'Köp inuti app'. One term is used everywhere, including the desktop fields.
- 'orankade' for 'casual' (multiplayer_online_body): accurate, but some Swedish players would simply say 'casual'.
- 'slumplandskap' for the 67 random landscapes is a transparent coined compound built on the game's own 'Landskap'; it is needed to fit shot_overlay_maps (27 of 28). Prose could alternatively say 'slumpmässiga landskap'.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal 'Utan reklam och köp i appen' (27 of 28): 'utan' is meant to govern both nouns, which is the normal reading; an unambiguous two-negation form does not fit in 28 characters.
- shot_caption_fair_deal 'Spelet som det installeras' is slightly stiff; at 99 of 100 characters there is no room for a smoother phrase.
- windows_search_term_3 'basbygge': 'basbyggande' or 'bygga bas' are plausible alternatives; no search data was checked.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are marked unchanged from v1 in the field map, but the translator had already altered them slightly from the committed v1 text ('Globulation 2 onlinetjänst' to the grammatical 'Globulation 2:s onlinetjänst', and 'Offline-spel/online-spel' to 'Offlinespel/onlinespel'). I kept these corrections; the Epic console text would need the same change to match.
- ai_guide: 'Setup guide' is now 'Guide för att komma igång' instead of 'Installationsguide', because nothing is installed (the server is built in). It is longer than the English label.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning, policy, protected-term or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; my 8 edits are naturalness and search-term improvements only. A native Swedish speaker has not validated any of this.

### sw — Swahili

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [sw.md](../translations/sw.md). Review record: [reviews/sw.json](../translations/reviews/sw.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (13): `fair_deal_line`, `ios_subtitle`, `ios_keywords`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `itch_downloads_note`, `trailer_line_3`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (6)**

- multiplayer_crossplay: desktop players were rendered 'Wachezaji wa kompyuta', the same construction the catalog uses for computer opponents ('wapinzani wa kompyuta'), so it could be read as CPU players sharing online games; reworded to 'Wachezaji walio kwenye kompyuta, kivinjari na vifaa vya mkononi' (players on a computer, browser and mobile devices). Same construction fixed in multiplayer_crossplay_apple.
- ios_subtitle: used 'RTS: mikakati ya koloni' while shot_overlay_hero, headline and play_short_description use 'Mikakati ya wakati halisi'; the glossary and field map require one genre term across these fields. Subtitle is now 'Mikakati ya wakati halisi' (25 chars) and 'RTS' moved into ios_keywords (now 99 bytes).
- trailer_line_3: 'Pambana na wapinzani kwa ardhi.' used 'kwa ardhi', which reads as 'by/with land' rather than 'for the land'; now 'Pigania ardhi na wapinzani.' (27 chars), echoing defense_heading.
- fair_deal_line / desktop_short_pitch / open_source_body: price word 'bure' (which can also mean 'in vain/worthless', notably in 'Ni wa bure') replaced with the unambiguous store term 'bila malipo'; desktop_short_pitch kept within 270 (267) by saying 'seva ya MCP ya mchezo' for the built-in MCP server.
- map_body / feature_maps: 'kutoka kwa mandhari' ('kutoka kwa' is used for people) corrected to 'kutoka kwenye'; 'Cheza ramani 23' corrected to 'Cheza kwenye ramani 23'.
- ai_body: calque 'Hii hufanya kazi' and the bare sentence 'Si lazima:' reworded ('Hili linawezekana...', 'Kipengele hiki si cha lazima: ...'); subject agreement fixed in 'Kipanya na kibodi yako vinaendelea'. All required qualifiers (optional, off unless turned on, only on your own computer, not included, complete without one) are intact.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- 'koloni' is kept for colony (from the reviewed v1 catalog; fits a creature colony), but the game's own Swahili UI calls colonies 'makazi'. A native speaker should decide whether the listing should match the in-game word, and whether 'koloni' carries an unwanted colonial-history connotation.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal ('Bila matangazo, bila ununuzi') and trailer_line_4 ('Bila matangazo. Bila ununuzi.') shorten 'in-app purchases' to plain 'ununuzi' (purchases): the store term 'ununuzi wa ndani ya programu' is 28 characters by itself and cannot fit beside 'no ads' in 28/32 characters. Both facts are kept, but the wording is slightly broader than the English.
- 'sarafu za kulipia' for premium currency follows the idiom 'za kulipia' = paid (as in 'huduma za kulipia'); a native speaker should confirm it does not read as 'coins used for paying'. 'loot boxes' is kept as the English loanword.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and embeds the loanword 'offline' ('Mikakati ya wakati halisi offline bila matangazo.'); natural in gamer usage but not polished standard Swahili. 'nje ya mtandao' / 'bila intaneti' do not fit with the colony-not-units contrast.
- shot_overlay_maps 'Ramani 23, aina 67 za nasibu' (23 maps, 67 random kinds) conveys the map generators only implicitly; 28 characters leaves no room for 'mandhari' plus 'random'.
- 'msimbo huria' for open source (also in windows_search_term_6) is consistent with the reviewed v1 legal text, but 'chanzo huria' / 'programu huria' are also in circulation; searchers may type the English term.
- 'Si lazima:' is used as the label for 'Optional:' (the usual Swahili UI rendering); 'Kwa hiari:' is an alternative a native marketer might prefer.
- 'wakala wa AI' for AI agent and 'AI' rather than 'akili bandia/akili mnemba' are the forms used in current Swahili tech writing but are recent coinages; ios_keywords and windows search terms are best guesses at Swahili search behaviour (many Swahili-speaking players search in English).
- shot_caption_fair_deal (99/100) lists its four items without a closing 'wala' because the conjunction would exceed the 100-character limit; shot_caption_ai_coplay is exactly 100.
- The review-record command printed no output; success was inferred from translations/reviews/sw.json being written and the strict check (--require-review) printing nothing for sw.
- Edits were applied with an exact-match replacement script rather than by hand; only translations/sw.md was modified by me (the review tool wrote translations/reviews/sw.json). No AI or human native-speaker validation has taken place.

### ta — Tamil

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ta.md](../translations/ta.md). Review record: [reviews/ta.json](../translations/reviews/ta.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (12): `listing_intro`, `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `defense_body`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `ai_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `shot_caption_economy`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (4)**

- map_body: '67 சீரற்ற நிலப்பரப்புகள்' reads most naturally as '67 uneven/rugged terrains' because சீரற்ற means irregular before a terrain noun; reworded to '67 நிலப்பரப்பு வகைகள்' producing a 'சீரற்ற வரைபடம்' (the game's own label for Random map), which keeps the 67 as landscape types and avoids '67 random maps'
- feature_maps: same 'uneven landscapes' misreading of the 67 random landscapes; fixed with the same wording as map_body
- amazon_tail: 'ஆன்லைன் பலர் ஆட்டம்' was an ungrammatical noun stack for 'online multiplayer'; now 'பலர் ஆடும் ஆன்லைன் ஆட்டம்', matching the game's own phrasing
- android_tail, ios_tail: missing sandhi in 'தொடுதிரை தளவமைப்புகள்'; now 'தனித்தனியான தொடுதிரைத் தளவமைப்புகள்'

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 say 'விளம்பரமோ பர்ச்சேஸோ இல்லை' (no ads or purchases): the full Google Play Tamil term 'இன்-ஆப்ஸ் பர்ச்சேஸ்கள்' (confirmed on Google Play Help in Tamil) cannot fit in 28/32 code points, so 'in-app' is dropped there. The full term is used in fair_deal_line, short_pitch, desktop_short_pitch and shot_caption_fair_deal. A native speaker should confirm the bare loanword reads as in-app purchases and not as a wider 'nothing is sold'.
- 'globs' is transliterated 'குளோப்கள்', which ties it to the product name but can be read as 'globes'; the alternative 'கிளாப்' reads as 'clap'. The game's Tamil strings give no precedent. Native check advised.
- 'premium currency' is rendered 'விலைக்கு வாங்கும் நாணயம்' (currency you buy for a price). Clear, but players may more often say 'பிரீமியம் கரன்சி'; native check advised.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 வரைபடம், 67 சீரற்ற வகை' (23 maps, 67 random kinds) is compressed to fit 28; 'map generators' is implied rather than stated, and nouns are singular after the numerals.
- shot_overlay_build / trailer_line_1 use the neuter pronoun 'அவை' for 'they' (no noun fits the limit); it is clear over a picture of the creatures but a native speaker should confirm it reads well on a title card.
- shot_overlay_hero is 'நிகழ்நேர உத்தி விளையாட்டு' (real-time strategy game) without 'colony', because the subtitle wording 'நிகழ்நேரக் குடியிருப்பு உத்தி' is 29 code points; same genre term, as the field map requires. In everyday Tamil 'குடியிருப்பு' also means housing quarters; it is kept because it is the game's own word for colony.
- 'Optional' is now expressed as 'விருப்பமிருந்தால்' (if you wish) and 'இது உங்கள் விருப்பத்தைப் பொறுத்தது' rather than the UI label 'விருப்பத்தேர்வு'; meaning is kept, but it is no longer the literal label word.
- ios_keywords (98 bytes) are Tamil-script terms plus RTS: RTS, war, army, offline, ads, without. Tamil players often search in English, and App Store Connect Tamil coverage is uncertain; search behaviour was not verified. windows_search_term_3 'தளம் கட்டுதல்' and _4 'குடியிருப்பு' are literal and unverified as real search phrases.
- Several fields sit at or within one code point of their limit (shot_caption_economy 100/100, four overlays at 28/28, desktop_short_pitch 267/270, play_short_description 79/80); any later edit must be recounted.
- Ranked is 'தரமதிப்பீட்டு', matching the game's own [hub ranked] label, though 'தரவரிசை' may be more familiar to players.
- multiplayer_lan_body adds the gloss '(LAN)' after 'உள்ளகப் பிணையம்' for clarity; not in the English but not a new claim.
- This is an AI review, not native-speaker validation. Web checks were limited to Google Play's Tamil pages (in-app purchases term; Strategy category = 'உத்தி').

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning errors of the maps-as-playing-cards class, no false claims, no price word in a forbidden field, no other platform in Apple-facing fields and no broken limit were found in the translator's draft; the remaining edits were naturalness fixes (stilted 'விருப்பத்தேர்வு:' label for Optional replaced by 'விருப்பமிருந்தால்,' / 'இது உங்கள் விருப்பத்தைப் பொறுத்தது'; ambiguous 'உண்டு' and 'அமையுங்கள்' replaced; 'host a room' aligned with the game's 'அறையை உருவாக்கு'; 'carry wheat to' made directional)

### te — Telugu

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [te.md](../translations/te.md). Review record: [reviews/te.json](../translations/reviews/te.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (5): `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `ai_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- map_body / feature_maps: the translator used the same verb (రూపొందించు) for both 'handmade' and 'generate', blurring the contrast between the 23 fixed maps and the 67 generators. I changed the generate side to సృష్టించు, which is also what the game's own 'Generate & play' button uses. A native speaker should confirm it reads well.
- feature_ai_coplay / ai_body: 'play the same colony' was literally 'కాలనీని ఆడండి / ఆడగలదు', which is awkward in Telugu. I changed it to నడపండి / నడపగలదు ('run the same colony together'), the listing's verb for 'command'. Same-side sense is kept; a native check is welcome.
- multiplayer_crossplay_apple: 'కలిసి ఆడతారు' could be read as players on other devices playing among themselves. Reworded to 'players on other devices also play in the same online games, on the same game version'.
- shot_overlay_maps: '23 మ్యాప్‌లు, 67 జనరేటర్లు' (26 chars) drops the word 'map' from 'map generators' because '67 మ్యాప్ జనరేటర్లు' is 33 characters. On its own జనరేటర్ can suggest an electrical generator in Telugu. I found no clearer wording within 28 characters; a native speaker should judge.
- Random 'landscapes' are rendered ప్రకృతి దృశ్యాలు, following the game's [Landscape] key as the glossary requires. It literally means 'nature scenery', and the game itself uses ల్యాండ్‌స్కేప్ in some other strings. A native speaker should confirm players will read it as a map type. Screen orientation uses different words (నిలువుగా / అడ్డంగా), so the two meanings do not collide.
- multiplayer_online_body uses the loanword క్యాజువల్ for 'casual', while the game's own UI labels that queue సాధారణ. I kept the loanword as the usual gaming term; decide whether the listing should match the in-game label.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal uses the shortened 'యాప్ కొనుగోళ్లు' (28 chars, exactly at the limit) because the standard Play term 'యాప్‌లో కొనుగోళ్లు' does not fit; trailer_line_4 uses the full term. Check that the short form is not read as 'the app need not be bought'.
- 'Premium currency' is 'డబ్బుతో కొనే కరెన్సీ' (currency bought with money) in fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal, consistent with the glossary. 'డబ్బు పెట్టి కొనే' might be more idiomatic but would push the caption over 100 characters.
- The genre term is the hyphenated loanword 'రియల్-టైమ్ స్ట్రాటజీ' throughout; windows_search_term_1 and ios_keywords use the native వ్యూహం to cover both search forms. Whether Telugu players type the hyphenated, unhyphenated or English form in store search needs native or search-data confirmation. వ్యూహం in ios_keywords is the native genre word (it also means tactics), not a literal item from the concept list.
- 'Globs' is transliterated గ్లాబ్ everywhere (description body only). The game's Telugu text has no established transliteration to check it against.
- Three fields sit exactly at their limit (play_short_description 80, shot_overlay_fair_deal 28, trailer_line_4 32), so any later edit to them must be recounted.
- The review file translations/reviews/te.json was rewritten by review-record (translator storefront-v2-translator-te, reviewer storefront-v2-reviewer-te, 78 source hashes). This is AI review only, not native-speaker validation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found: translations/te.md already held a complete storefront-v2 translation (78 fields, same order as en.md), not the v1 text the task described. My five edits are wording improvements only.

### th — Thai

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [th.md](../translations/th.md). Review record: [reviews/th.json](../translations/reviews/th.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (10): `listing_intro`, `economy_body`, `ai_body`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `windows_search_term_7`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `itch_downloads_note`, `shot_caption_maps`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- ai_body: 'You bring your own AI agent' was 'คุณต้องใช้เอเจนต์ AI ของคุณเอง' (you must use your own AI agent), which sat awkwardly beside 'optional'; changed to 'คุณต้องมี…' (you need to have your own). A native speaker should confirm it no longer reads as an obligation.
- Genre term: 'เกมวางแผนเรียลไทม์' is used throughout, with 'เกมวางแผนอาณานิคมเรียลไทม์' for ios_subtitle and shot_overlay_hero. A native marketer should confirm this is preferred over 'เกมกลยุทธ์เรียลไทม์' / 'เกมวางแผนกลยุทธ์แบบเรียลไทม์' for store search.
- computer opponent = 'คู่ต่อสู้คอมพิวเตอร์' (kept distinct from 'เอเจนต์ AI'). Thai players often say 'บอท'; a native speaker may prefer it, but it would have to change in every field and still fit the 28-character overlay.
- premium currency = 'ไม่มีเงินในเกมที่ต้องเติมเงินซื้อ' (no in-game money that has to be bought by topping up) in fair_deal_line and shot_caption_fair_deal. Reads as natural gamer Thai to me; a native check that it cannot be read as 'the game has no resources' is worthwhile.
- in-app purchases = 'การซื้อในแอป' (Google Play's Thai term). Apple's Thai pages may use 'การซื้อภายในแอป'; I could not check the live stores. The shorter form is needed for the 28-character overlay, which is exactly at the limit.
- handmade maps = 'แผนที่ทำมือ' ('ทำมือ' is normally used for crafts); understandable, but a native speaker might prefer 'แผนที่ที่ออกแบบด้วยมือ' in map_body and feature_maps.
- shot_overlay_maps '23 แผนที่ 67 ตัวสร้างแผนที่' puts the numbers before the nouns (marketing style, not standard classifier order), and 'ตัวสร้างแผนที่' for 'map generators' is a literal coinage. Fits at 27/28; worth a native glance.
- ios_keywords 'RTS,กลยุทธ์,ออฟไลน์,ไม่มีโฆษณา,สงคราม' (97 of 100 bytes) is chosen from judgement, not App Store search-volume data. How Apple tokenises unspaced Thai keywords is unverified.
- windows_search_term_7: changed to 'เกม MCP AI agent' with 'AI agent' in Latin letters, on my judgement that Thai users type the English term when searching. The visible copy still uses 'เอเจนต์ AI'. Revert if consistency is preferred.
- Three fields sit exactly at their limit: play_short_description (80/80), shot_overlay_fair_deal (28/28), shot_overlay_ai_coplay (28/28). Any later wording change must be recounted.
- Thai has no word spaces, so the screenshot and trailer renderer needs Thai-aware line breaking; the catalog contains no zero-width spaces. Years are written 'ค.ศ. 2001' and 'ค.ศ. 2004' to keep the protected digits, not converted to the Buddhist era.
- mac_tail uses 'Apple Silicon' with a capital S, as I recall Apple's Thai pages writing it; not verified against the live site.
- epic_tail, epic_legal and windows_search_term_1 keep their storefront-v1 text unchanged, as the field map requires. I confirmed they are identical to the v1 text and did not re-edit them.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; all ten edits were naturalness or precision fixes. translations/th.md already held a storefront-v2 translation (78 fields, correct order) when I opened it, not the v1 text the task described.

### tr — Turkish

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [tr.md](../translations/tr.md). Review record: [reviews/tr.json](../translations/reviews/tr.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (13): `economy_body`, `control_body`, `defense_body`, `features_heading`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `shot_overlay_build`, `shot_caption_build`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (10)**

- shot_caption_ai_coplay: the participle 'bağlanan' attached to 'oyun', so the caption read as 'a local game that connects through the MCP server' instead of the AI agent being the thing connected; restructured so the agent is what connects (97/100).
- multiplayer_crossplay: 'Masaüstü, tarayıcı ve mobil oyuncular' was ungrammatical as a noun compound (reads like 'desktop players' without the required possessive); rewritten as 'Masaüstünde, tarayıcıda ve mobil cihazlarda oynayanlar'.
- multiplayer_online_body: 'bir bilgisayar rakibiyle oynarsınız' could be read as playing together with a computer opponent; changed to 'bir bilgisayar rakibine karşı oynarsınız' (against).
- ai_body: 'ajanınızı siz getirirsiniz' was a calque of 'bring your own'; changed to 'siz sağlarsınız'. All required qualifiers (optional, off unless turned on, only on your own computer, not included, complete without one) were verified present.
- shot_caption_build: faulty coordination of a noun and a clause ('Bir bina ve ona kaç işçi verileceğini seçin'); rewritten as two imperatives (96/100).
- shot_overlay_build: caption had an internal full stop but no final one ('Karar sizin. İnşaat onlardan'); changed to 'Karar sizin, inşaat onlardan' (28/28). trailer_line_1 keeps the same words as two sentences.
- features_heading: 'Oyunda neler var.' is an interrogative form ending in a full stop, which reads as a typo in Turkish; changed to 'Oyunda neler var?'.
- android_tail / ios_tail: 'dokunmatik düzenler' was unclear for 'touch layouts'; changed to 'dokunmatik arayüz düzenleri'.
- control_body / defense_body: 'bayrak koyun' replaced by the idiomatic collocation 'bayrak dikin' (also avoids the koyun = 'sheep' homograph).
- economy_body: stacked semicolon plus colon in one sentence split into two sentences. open_source_body: added the explicit subject 'Oyun,' to the licence sentence.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (14)**

- 'manzara' for the 67 random landscape types (map_body, feature_maps) follows the game's own Turkish string [Landscape] = Manzara as the glossary requires, but to a newcomer it means 'scenery/view'; a native speaker should confirm it reads clearly as a kind of generated map.
- 'Otel' for inn (economy_body, defense_body, shot_caption_economy) also follows the game's own string; it literally means 'hotel'. defense_body adds the gloss 'orduyu besleyen otellerde' (the inns that feed the army) to keep the sentence understandable; this is an explanation, not a new claim.
- 'globül' for globs follows the in-game Turkish tutorial text; kept consistently and only in the four fields whose English has 'globs'.
- shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 use 'oyun içi alım' (in-game purchase) because the store term 'uygulama içi satın alma' cannot fit in 28/32 characters together with 'no ads'. The full store term is used in fair_deal_line, short_pitch, desktop_short_pitch and shot_caption_fair_deal.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay / trailer_line_ai_coplay: 'Yapay zekâ ajanınız takımda' ('your AI agent is on the team') conveys same-side play within 28 characters but is looser than 'play alongside'; the literal 'ajanınızla oynayın' is 29 characters.
- play_short_description is exactly 80 characters and omits the customary comma after 'değil' ('Birimleri değil koloniyi yönetin.') to keep both 'çevrimdışı' and 'reklamsız'; acceptable in short copy, but a native editor may prefer another trade-off.
- ios_subtitle 'Gerçek zamanlı strateji oyunu' (29/30) drops 'colony' because 'Gerçek zamanlı koloni stratejisi' is 31 characters; shot_overlay_hero uses the same genre term.
- ios_keywords (100/100 bytes) are judgment calls on Turkish search behaviour: 'internetsiz' and 'offline' both cover offline, 'üs,kurma' covers base building, 'çatışma' stands for skirmish. Worth checking against real App Store search data.
- windows_search_term_7 writes 'yapay zeka' without the circumflex on purpose (how users type it), while visible copy uses the TDK spelling 'yapay zekâ'.
- epic_legal (unchanged from the reviewed v1 text saved in the Epic console) has 'Resmi' without the circumflex while the game uses 'Resmî'; left untouched because the field is marked unchanged.
- 'ganimet kutusu' (loot box), 'gündelik' (casual queue, the game's own term) and 'bilgisayar rakibi' (computer opponent; the game UI itself says 'yapay zekâ rakipleri', deliberately avoided per the glossary) should be confirmed by a native-speaking gamer.
- Register is formal 'siz' throughout, matching the game's own UI strings; some Turkish mobile listings use informal 'sen'.
- Process note: review-record was run twice. The first run happened just before my last two edits (shot_caption_ai_coplay, open_source_body) because a scratch script aborted; I re-ran review-record after the final edit, so the recorded hashes match the final file (reviews/tr.json: 78 source and 78 translation hashes). translations/reviews/tr.json was modified by the tool as intended.
- No human or native-speaker validation has taken place; this is an AI review of an AI translation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning-reversing errors, false claims, forbidden price words, broken limits or damaged protected terms were found in the storefront-v2 draft; every limit was re-counted by code point (UTF-8 bytes for ios_keywords) and the field set and order match en.md (78/78). The fixes below are grammar and clarity defects, not policy violations.

### uk — Ukrainian

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [uk.md](../translations/uk.md). Review record: [reviews/uk.json](../translations/reviews/uk.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (14): `ios_keywords`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `feature_maps`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `ai_body`, `itch_downloads_note`, `shot_caption_fair_deal`, `shot_caption_ai_coplay`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (11)**

- In-app purchases: rendered as «покупки в грі» in every field (translator's choice, which I kept). This is a store-neutral term, not the exact label of either store; I believe Google Play uses «покупки в додатку» and the App Store «покупки в програмі», but I could not verify the live store labels. The longer standard «внутрішньоігрові покупки» does not fit short_pitch, the overlay or the trailer line. A native speaker should confirm this reads as the store concept.
- ios_keywords: changed to «RTS,офлайн,без реклами,без інтернету,будівництво,війна» (98 bytes). I added «без інтернету» as the phrase Ukrainian players commonly type for offline games and dropped «база», «армія», «бій» to make room. This is my judgement, not backed by App Store search-volume data; someone with ASO data for Ukraine should check it.
- play_short_description drops "offline" per the field map's cut order (77/80 characters). «Офлайн-стратегія в реальному часі без реклами. Керуйте колонією, а не юнітами.» would fit at 78 but weakens the "not every unit" contrast; a human may prefer that trade.
- shot_overlay_maps «23 карти, 67 генераторів» leaves "of maps" implied, because «67 генераторів карт» is 29 characters, 1 over the limit. Check that it reads clearly on the rendered screenshot.
- «преміум-валюта» is kept for "premium currency"; a native gamer should confirm it is understood as currency bought with real money and not as an in-game resource.
- «діловитих істот» for "busy creatures" in listing_intro is a stylistic choice a native speaker should confirm.
- I changed «сервер MCP» to «MCP-сервер» throughout (five fields) as the more usual form in Ukrainian tech writing; this is a preference, not a correction.
- mac_tail uses «Mac із процесором Apple (Apple silicon)»; Apple's current Ukrainian wording (процесор or чип) was not verified against Apple's site.
- shot_overlay_ai_coplay and trailer_line_ai_coplay drop "your" («Грайте разом із ШІ-агентом») to fit the limit; "your own" is kept in ai_heading and the body fields.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are byte-identical to the reviewed storefront-v1 text and were not re-edited.
- The plain `check` only runs the composed per-store length checks when no locale has errors, and other locales still fail. I therefore measured the Ukrainian composed descriptions directly with the tool's own functions: Google Play and F-Droid 3089 of 4000 characters, App Store 3014, Mac App Store 2938, Microsoft 3187, Epic 2744, with no failures.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found in the translator's draft; my edits are naturalness and clarity improvements. The catalog preamble (provenance note) was also updated to describe them.

### ur — Urdu

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [ur.md](../translations/ur.md). Review record: [reviews/ur.json](../translations/reviews/ur.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (17): `listing_intro`, `control_heading`, `control_body`, `economy_body`, `map_body`, `feature_colonies`, `feature_scripting`, `feature_ai_coplay`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `open_source_body`, `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `mac_tail`, `amazon_tail`, `shot_caption_economy`, `shot_caption_territory`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (9)**

- shot_caption_economy: 'چھتوں' (oblique plural of the game's word for swarm, چھتہ) is spelled identically to 'roofs' (plural of چھت), so the standalone caption could read 'workers carry wheat to the inns and roofs'; rewritten with the unambiguous form 'سرائے اور چھتے تک'
- listing_intro, control_heading, control_body, economy_body: 'globs' was transliterated 'گلوب', which is the ordinary Urdu word for 'globe' and differs from the creature name the game's own Urdu text uses ('گلوبیول', 13 occurrences in texts.ur.txt); changed to 'گلوبیول' everywhere so the listing matches the game
- map_body: '23 نقشے کھیلیں' ('play 23 maps' as if maps were the thing played) changed to '23 نقشوں پر کھیلیں' (play on 23 maps)
- feature_ai_coplay, ai_body: 'بستی کھیلیں / کھیل سکتا ہے' (literally 'play a colony', unidiomatic and unclear) changed to 'بستی چلائیں' / 'آپ کے ساتھ مل کر آپ ہی کی بستی چلا سکتا ہے', which keeps the same-side, same-colony meaning
- multiplayer_online_body: 'کمپیوٹر حریف سے کھیلتے ہیں' was ambiguous between playing with and against; now 'کمپیوٹر حریف کے خلاف'. 'درجہ بند کھیلنے' made explicit as 'درجہ بند میچ کھیلنے'
- feature_colonies: awkward 'ایک ہی میچ میں 16 بستیوں تک' reworded to 'ایک ہی میچ میں 16 تک بستیاں'
- shot_caption_territory: 'کہاں ملتی ہیں' could read 'where rival colonies are found'; now 'کہاں ایک دوسرے سے ملتی ہیں' (where they meet each other)
- open_source_body: 'کا آغاز 2001 میں ہوا' tightened to 'پر کام 2001 میں شروع ہوا' (work began in 2001, per glossary), and opened with an Urdu word
- RTL integrity: listing_intro, ai_body, open_source_body, android_tail, ios_tail, mac_tail, amazon_tail and feature_scripting began with a Latin-script term, which can flip paragraph direction where a store auto-detects direction from the first strong character; each now opens with an Urdu word without changing meaning

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (12)**

- Creature name: I chose the game's own Urdu term 'گلوبیول' (globule) over the transliteration 'گلوب' (reads as 'globe'). A native speaker should confirm this is the preferred name; it is used consistently in the four body fields that have 'globs' in English.
- In-app purchases: body fields use 'درون ایپ خریداری' (the term I believe Google Play shows in Urdu; not verified against a live Urdu Play page). shot_overlay_fair_deal and trailer_line_4 use the plainer 'ایپ میں خریداری' because 'درون ایپ' does not fit 28 characters with 'no ads'. Same fact, two wordings.
- 'پریمیم کرنسی' (loanword) and 'لوٹ باکس' are kept as gamer loanwords; a native reviewer may prefer a plainer 'no currency to buy' wording for premium currency.
- 'فوری میچ' for quick match matches the game's own Urdu UI label, but 'فوری' literally means 'immediate', which sits close to the glossary's warning against words that promise speed. 'درجہ بند' (ranked), 'عام' (casual) and 'کمرہ' (room) also follow the game's UI rather than the loanwords (رینکڈ, کیژول) many Urdu-speaking gamers use.
- Genre term is 'ریئل ٹائم حکمت عملی' throughout (without the izafat zer the game text uses, to save characters in 28-character fields). Many Urdu gamers would say 'اسٹریٹیجی'; windows_search_term_1 keeps the reviewed v1 variant 'حقیقی وقت کی حکمت عملی' as the field map instructs for the renamed field.
- ios_subtitle / shot_overlay_hero 'بستی کی ریئل ٹائم حکمت عملی' is a genitive ('the colony's real-time strategy'); acceptable but a native marketer might find a punchier form.
- ios_keywords and windows_search_term_\* are best-guess Urdu-script search terms; Urdu-speaking players often search in English, and the App Store has no Urdu localisation, so these could not be validated against real search behaviour. 'اڈے کی تعمیر' was kept for base building because the loanword 'بیس بلڈنگ' reads as 'twenty buildings'.
- epic_legal (unchanged v1 field, left untouched) uses 'سرکاری' for 'official', which also means 'governmental'; the game's Urdu UI uses the same word, so I left it, but 'باضابطہ' may be better. It also still begins with the Latin product name, as do trailer_title and itch_downloads_note.
- 'بستی کو بھوکا نہ رہنے دیں' (Keep the colony fed) is phrased negatively ('do not let the colony go hungry') and uses default masculine agreement with the feminine 'بستی'; common usage, but worth a native check.
- 'منظرنامہ' for the 67 random landscapes follows the game's own translation, though in everyday Urdu it means 'scenario'.
- The review-record command printed no output; I confirmed success by the presence of translations/reviews/ur.json and a clean strict check (check --require-review) for ur.
- No human or native-speaker validation has been done.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No limit, protected-term, price-word or Apple-platform violations were found in the draft; all tight fields were recounted (play_short_description 79/80, ios_subtitle 27/30, ios_keywords 99/100 bytes, short_pitch 158/170, desktop_short_pitch 248/270, overlays 16-28/28, trailer lines 25-30/32)

### vi — Vietnamese

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [vi.md](../translations/vi.md). Review record: [reviews/vi.json](../translations/reviews/vi.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (4): `android_tail`, `ios_tail`, `ai_body`, `multiplayer_online_body`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (0)**

- None reported.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (10)**

- shot_overlay_fair_deal 'Không quảng cáo & mua in-app' (28/28): the negation is meant to cover both items, but a reader could parse it as 'no ads, and in-app purchases'. No unambiguous wording with the App Store term fits 28 characters ('Không quảng cáo hay mua in-app' is 30). A native speaker should judge it; a swap to 'Không mua in-app & quảng cáo' (28) is less ambiguous but stops leading with ads.
- 'thuộc địa' for colony follows the game's own Vietnamese strings (texts.vi.txt) but carries a colonial-empire connotation in Vietnamese rather than an ant-colony one. A native speaker should confirm it is acceptable as the headline word; changing it would require changing the game translation too.
- 'tiền tệ trả phí' for premium currency (fair_deal_line, shot_caption_fair_deal) is understandable and used in Vietnamese game press, but players more often talk about 'nạp tiền'; a native marketer should confirm it reads as 'currency bought with real money'.
- Genre term: the listing uses 'chiến lược thời gian thực' (the encyclopedic RTS term), while many Vietnamese players search 'game chiến thuật'. 'chiến thuật' is covered only in ios_keywords and windows_search_term_5; a native speaker should confirm the headline/subtitle choice.
- ios_keywords is exactly 100/100 bytes with fully accented words. It repeats the syllable 'chiến' from the subtitle (in 'chiến thuật'), and I could not verify how the App Store tokenises or diacritic-folds Vietnamese, so the keyword choice is unvalidated against real search data.
- 'tác nhân AI' for AI agent is the common tech-press rendering, but some readers know only the English 'AI agent' (used in windows_search_term_7). Overlay and trailer line 'Chơi cùng tác nhân AI' drop 'your' to fit and to stay identical to each other.
- 'Bạn quyết định. Chúng xây.' (shot_overlay_build, trailer_line_1) uses the pronoun 'chúng' for the creatures; correct, but slightly impersonal when read with no picture. A noun version ('Thuộc địa xây') exceeds 28 characters.
- 'lo phần việc' for 'does the work' (control_heading, short_pitch, desktop_short_pitch, feature_control) is understandable and consistent but a little stiff; I left it because the alternatives either add 'the rest' or lose the decide/work contrast.
- shot_caption_fair_deal is exactly 100/100 and play_short_description exactly 80/80 (it drops 'offline' per the cut order and has no final full stop); windows search terms use exactly 21 distinct words. Any later edit to these must be recounted.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are byte-identical to the reviewed storefront-v1 text in git HEAD; they say 'Chính sách bảo mật' for privacy policy, where Apple's Vietnamese uses 'quyền riêng tư'. Left unchanged as instructed by the field map.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No serious meaning, policy or limit errors were found: the catalog at translations/vi.md already held a storefront-v2 translation (not v1 as the task text said), and my four edits are polish only (screen orientation reworded to 'màn hình dọc / màn hình ngang' in android_tail and ios_tail; 'complete without one' in ai_body changed from 'khi thiếu nó' to 'khi không có tác nhân AI'; 'instead' restored in multiplayer_online_body). I also added a short reviewer note to the preamble.

### zh-Hans — Chinese — Simplified

Rating: **Publishable with concerns**. Catalog: [zh-Hans.md](../translations/zh-Hans.md). Review record: [reviews/zh-Hans.json](../translations/reviews/zh-Hans.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (11): `short_pitch`, `desktop_short_pitch`, `ios_keywords`, `map_body`, `feature_tutorial`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `ai_body`, `epic_tail`, `epic_legal`, `shot_overlay_build`, `trailer_line_1`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (2)**

- epic_tail: 'creates an account' was rendered as '需要创建账号' (the player must create an account); corrected to '并会创建一个账号' (online play creates one), matching the English and epic_legal. This field is marked unchanged from storefront-v1, so the error was carried over from v1.
- multiplayer_online_body, epic_tail, epic_legal: 'guest' was 访客 (visitor), but the game's own zh-cn interface says 游客, which is also the standard Mainland gaming term; aligned all three to 游客 so the listing matches what the player sees. Terminology mismatch, not a meaning error.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (13)**

- fair_deal_line / shot_caption_fair_deal: 'loot boxes' is 开箱抽奖. It is clear, but a native speaker should confirm it against 战利品箱 or 抽卡 as the term Mainland players expect.
- fair_deal_line / shot_caption_fair_deal: 'premium currency' is 付费货币, the usual player term; confirm it reads as currency bought with real money and not as 'the game has no resources'.
- In-app purchases is 应用内购买 everywhere (Google Play and Microsoft term). Apple's own Mainland wording is 'App 内购买'; it is understood on the App Store but not Apple's exact string.
- ios_keywords: I swapped 军队 for 对战 (skirmish/versus) as a stronger search term, and kept 策略 and 单机 as the Mainland search forms of the RTS and offline concepts. These are not literal items on the field map's concept list and the choice is not validated against real App Store search data.
- Account is 账号 throughout the listing (the usual word in Mainland game listings), while the game's interface uses 账户. Both are correct; a native reviewer may prefer to align them.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are marked unchanged from storefront-v1 in the field map, but I edited both (游客, and the account sentence). If the v1 text is already saved in the Epic console, it now differs from the catalog.
- open_source_body: 'over two decades' is 二十多年间 ('across twenty-plus years'), consistent with 2001 to 2026 but slightly more specific than the English.
- shot_overlay_build / trailer_line_1: shortened to '你做决定，工人来建' for rhythm; 'they' is rendered as 工人 (workers) per the field map. A native speaker should confirm the punch.
- trailer_title: 玩法预告片 for 'gameplay trailer'; 实机预告片 is an equally common Mainland alternative.
- shot_overlay_maps: 67 种地图生成器 is literal and clear but slightly technical.
- Handmade maps is 手工地图, following the game's own string; it is a little terse as marketing copy.
- short_pitch / desktop_short_pitch: 'You set the priorities' is now '先做什么由你定' ('what gets done first is up to you') instead of the stiffer 优先顺序; the game's interface uses 优先级 for the same idea.
- Not validated by a human or native speaker; this is an AI review of an AI translation.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No errors of the class found in past reviews (maps as playing cards, tabletop game, 'free' losing its price meaning, 'opponents on a computer') were present. No price word in a forbidden field, no other platform in Apple-facing fields, all protected terms and numbers intact, all limits respected (largest overlay 16/28, ios_keywords 99/100 bytes).

### zh-Hant — Chinese — Traditional

Rating: **Publishable**. Catalog: [zh-Hant.md](../translations/zh-Hant.md). Review record: [reviews/zh-Hant.json](../translations/reviews/zh-Hant.json).

Fields the reviewer changed (8): `defense_body`, `feature_control`, `multiplayer_online_body`, `multiplayer_crossplay`, `multiplayer_crossplay_apple`, `shot_caption_hero`, `shot_caption_build`, `shot_caption_battle`.

**Serious errors found and fixed (5)**

- defense_body, feature_control, shot_caption_hero, shot_caption_battle: 'warriors' was 戰士, but the game's own zh-tw strings call the unit 士兵 ([Warrior]/[warriors]); changed so the listing matches what the player sees in game (terminology mismatch, not a meaning error).
- multiplayer_crossplay, multiplayer_crossplay_apple: the literal 都在相同的遊戲版本上一起玩同樣的線上對局 was awkward and blurred that a matching game version is the condition for sharing matches (claims record R6); rewritten as 只要遊戲版本相同，就能在同一場線上對局中一起玩.
- multiplayer_online_body: 積分賽或休閒的 1v1 快速配對 was ungrammatical (休閒 left dangling as a modifier); rewritten as 上線進行 1v1 快速配對，可以選積分賽或休閒賽.
- shot_caption_build: the animal pronoun 牠們 referred back to 工人 (workers), which reads oddly in a caption that stands alone; replaced with the noun 工人.
- shot_caption_battle: 敵對殖民地的戰士 could read as only the enemy's warriors; now 來自敵對殖民地的士兵，為同一片土地而戰.

**Residual concerns for a native speaker (10)**

- In-app purchases is 應用程式內購買 throughout (the Microsoft Store wording, one character longer than Google Play's 應用程式內購). The App Store in Taiwan says App 內購買, so short_pitch as Apple promotional text does not use Apple's exact term. A native speaker should decide whether the Apple-facing text needs Apple's wording, and whether the overlay and trailer line would read better with the colloquial 無廣告、無內購.
- Loot boxes is 戰利品箱 and premium currency is 付費貨幣. Both are accurate, but Taiwanese players also say 轉蛋/抽獎箱 and 課金貨幣/儲值貨幣; a native speaker should confirm the most recognisable choice.
- AI agent is 'AI 代理' (kept clearly distinct from 電腦對手 for computer opponent). Taiwan usage is not settled between AI 代理, AI 代理人 and the English 'AI Agent'; a native check is worthwhile, including for windows_search_term_7.
- ios_keywords (96 of 100 bytes): RTS,策略,基地,建造,單機,離線,無廣告,戰爭,戰鬥,對戰,多人連線,戰術,經營. 策略 (strategy, the form players search instead of 戰略) and 經營 (management, standing in for 'economy') are loose mappings of the allowed concepts; LAN, map editor and army are left out for space. Not validated against real App Store search data.
- windows_search_term_5 renders 'offline strategy' as 單機策略遊戲 (單機 is the usual Taiwanese search word for offline/standalone games, but it also implies single-player). 離線策略遊戲 is the literal alternative.
- open_source_body keeps 二十年來 for 'over two decades', spelled as a word like the English; it carries the same mild implication of continuity as the English does.
- Units are 士兵 and landscapes are 隨機地形類型, following the game's zh-tw strings; the game itself calls its computer players 'AI 對手', while the listing uses 電腦對手 as the glossary requires, so the listing and in-game menu differ on that one term.
- epic_tail and epic_legal are byte-identical to the reviewed storefront-v1 text and were not restyled (they say 離線遊戲/無需註冊帳號 where the v2 fields say 離線遊玩/不需要帳號).
- The catalog file was already storefront-v2 when I opened it, and the preamble already carried the required provenance, so I did not rewrite it beyond adding 士兵 to its list of game terms.
- The review is AI-only; no native human speaker has validated the text.

**Reviewer's overall statement**

- No meaning-reversing errors, false claims, policy violations or broken limits were found. The catalog already held a complete storefront-v2 translation, not v1 text; all 78 headings matched en.md, and the price word 免費 appears only in fair_deal_line, desktop_short_pitch, open_source_body and epic_legal, where it is allowed.

## Caption pass — 2026-11-24

### Method

- **Why.** The first captioned-screenshot renders set whole locales well below full type size, because one long `shot_overlay_*` caption sets the size for every slide of its locale ([renderer README](../assets/storefront/tools/README.md#fit-the-caption-editors-check)). This pass shortened and checked those captions only.
- **Per-script limits.** `translations/platforms.json` now gives screenshot captions a length limit and target per script (`field_limits`), and the mobile gallery order (`asset_text.mobile_gallery_order`: hero, build, fair_deal, battle, economy, territory, maps, opponents); `storefront.py`, its tests, the [field map](../translations/storefront-field-map.md) and the [translations README](../translations/README.md) follow them.
- **Legibility gate.** Each locale was checked with the renderer's `screenshots.py fit --locale <code> --min-scale 0.85`, which fails when either phone-portrait set (`apple-iphone-6.9`, `google-play-phone`) is set below 85% of full caption size. Tablet sets are reported but do not count.
- **Editor and independent reviewer per locale.** Each of the 44 locales had a caption editor and then a separate caption reviewer. Both edited only `shot_overlay_*` fields. The reviewer back-translated every caption, ran the gate and re-recorded the hash-bound review with `storefront.py review-record`; the records in [`translations/reviews/`](../translations/reviews/) now name `storefront-v2-caption-editor-<code>` as translator and `storefront-v2-caption-reviewer-<code>` as reviewer. As before, this is AI editing with independent AI review, not native-speaker validation.
- **How this section was made.** It is generated by script from the caption reviewers' structured results (per locale: fields the reviewer changed, errors fixed, residual concerns, gate passed). Reviewer text is verbatim, with the same mechanical changes as above: `*` escaped and the absolute local repository path shortened to a repository-relative one. "Reviewer changed" counts only the reviewer's own edits; "Changed in pass" counts the `shot_overlay_*` fields that differ from the previous commit, from editor and reviewer together, measured from the catalogs. The script also confirmed that no field other than `shot_overlay_*` changed in any locale catalog.

### English changes

Three English captions were shortened, and [`source-hashes.json`](../translations/source-hashes.json) was refreshed for them:

| Field | Before | After |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `shot_overlay_maps` | 23 maps, 67 map generators | 23 maps, or random ones |
| `shot_overlay_opponents` | Face 8 computer opponents | 8 computer opponents |
| `shot_overlay_fair_deal` | No ads, no in-app purchases | No ads. No in-app buys |

English now renders at 95% of full caption size on iPhone and 97% on Android phone. The overlay no longer carries the count of 67 landscapes; the [glossary](../translations/glossary.md) was updated to match.

### Results

- **Gate.** 41 of 44 locales pass the 85% gate. Below it: `eu`, `sw`, `ta`.
- **Reviews re-recorded.** 44 of 44; `storefront.py check --require-review` validates all 45 canonical locales.
- **Fields changed by reviewers.** 24 in 15 locales. Changed in the pass as a whole (editor and reviewer): 202 fields in 44 locales.
- **Errors fixed by reviewers.** 27 entries in 15 locales.
- **Residual concerns.** 166 entries across 44 locales.
